### [1] [PROMISE: Proof Automation as Structural Imitation of Human Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05399)
*Youngjoo Ahn,Sangyeop Yeo,Gijung Lim,Jongmin Lee,Jinyoung Yeo,Jieung Kim*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: PROMISE框架通过结构感知的证明挖掘方法，将证明生成重构为基于证明状态转换的状态搜索，显著提升了形式化软件验证中的自动证明生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在自然语言处理、视觉和代码生成方面表现良好，但形式化验证仍然需要大量人工努力。交互式定理证明需要严格逻辑约束下的手动证明构建，限制了可扩展性（如seL4微内核验证耗费数十年）。现有基于LLM的方法主要依赖单次生成或浅层检索，难以扩展到具有深层结构依赖的大型、相互依赖的验证任务。

Method: 提出PROMISE（PROof MIning via Structural Embeddings）框架，将证明生成重构为基于证明状态转换的状态搜索。不同于表面级检索，PROMISE从证明状态和策略转换中挖掘结构模式，在迭代搜索过程中检索和适配兼容的证明片段。

Result: 在seL4基准测试中，PROMISE在多个LLM后端上始终优于先前系统（如Selene和Rango），实现了高达+26分的改进（相对增益186%），同时在不同模型间保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 结构感知的证明挖掘方法对于可扩展的定理证明具有显著效果，PROMISE框架通过挖掘和重用证明结构模式，有效提升了形式化验证中自动证明生成的性能。

Abstract: Automated proof generation for formal software verification remains largely unresolved despite advances in large language models (LLMs). While LLMs perform well in NLP, vision, and code generation, formal verification still requires substantial human effort. Interactive theorem proving (ITP) demands manual proof construction under strict logical constraints, limiting scalability; for example, verifying the seL4 microkernel required decades of effort.
  Existing LLM-based approaches attempt to automate this process but remain limited. Most rely on single-shot generation or shallow retrieval, which may work for small proofs but fail to scale to large, interdependent verification tasks with deep structural dependencies.
  We present PROMISE (PROof MIning via Structural Embeddings), a structure-aware framework that reframes proof generation as a stateful search over proof-state transitions. Instead of surface-level retrieval, PROMISE mines structural patterns from proof states and tactic transitions, enabling retrieval and adaptation of compatible proof fragments during iterative search.
  We evaluate PROMISE on the seL4 benchmark across multiple LLM backends and compare it with prior systems such as Selene and Rango. PROMISE consistently outperforms prior methods, achieving up to +26 point improvements (186% relative gain) while maintaining robustness across models, demonstrating the effectiveness of structure-aware proof mining for scalable theorem proving.

</details>

### [2] [Can You Trust the Vectors in Your Vector Database? Black-Hole Attack from Embedding Space Defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05480)
*Hanxi Li,Jianan Zhou,Jiale Lao,Yibo Wang,Zhengmao Ye,Yang Cao,Junfen Wang,Mingjie Tang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出"黑洞攻击"——一种针对向量数据库的投毒攻击，通过在存储向量几何中心附近注入少量恶意向量，使其像黑洞一样吸引查询，频繁出现在检索结果中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量数据库作为现代AI应用的检索骨干，其安全性尚未得到充分研究。作者发现高维嵌入空间中的几何缺陷使得检索系统天生脆弱，需要揭示这种安全风险。

Method: 提出黑洞攻击：利用"中心性驱动的枢纽性"现象，在向量数据库的几何中心附近注入恶意向量。这些中心区域在实际中几乎为空，但靠近中心的向量会成为大量其他向量的最近邻。

Result: 实验显示恶意向量在top-10检索结果中出现率高达99.85%。现有枢纽性缓解方法要么显著降低检索准确率，要么只能提供有限保护，表明需要更鲁棒的防御机制。

Conclusion: 向量数据库中的向量不能盲目信任：高维嵌入的几何缺陷使检索系统天生脆弱。黑洞攻击暴露了向量检索系统的安全漏洞，现有防御方法不足，需要开发更有效的安全机制。

Abstract: Vector databases serve as the retrieval backbone of modern AI applications, yet their security remains largely unexplored. We propose the Black-Hole Attack, a poisoning attack that injects a small number of malicious vectors near the geometric center of the stored vectors. These injected vectors attract queries like a black hole and frequently appear in the top-k retrieval results for most queries. This attack is enabled by a phenomenon we term centrality-driven hubness: in high-dimensional embedding spaces, vectors near the centroid become nearest neighbors of a disproportionately large number of other vectors, while this centroid region is nearly empty in practice. The attack shows that vectors in a vector database cannot be blindly trusted: geometric defects in high-dimensional embeddings make retrieval inherently vulnerable. Our experiments show that malicious vectors appear in up to 99.85% of top-10 results. Additionally, we evaluate existing hubness mitigation methods as potential defenses against the Black-Hole Attack. The results show that these methods either significantly reduce retrieval accuracy or provide limited protection, which indicates the need for more robust defenses against the Black-Hole Attack.

</details>


### [3] [SkillAttack: Automated Red Teaming of Agent Skills through Attack Path Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04989)
*Zenghao Duan,Yuxin Tian,Zhiyi Yin,Liang Pang,Jingcheng Deng,Zihao Wei,Shicheng Xu,Yuyao Ge,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SkillAttack是一个红队框架，通过对抗性提示动态验证LLM技能漏洞的可利用性，无需修改技能本身即可利用非恶意技能中的潜在漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体系统依赖开放注册的技能库扩展能力，但开放生态使技能难以全面审查。现有攻击依赖注入恶意指令，容易被静态审计检测。然而，非恶意技能也可能存在潜在漏洞，攻击者仅通过对抗性提示即可利用，无需修改技能本身。

Method: SkillAttack结合漏洞分析、表面并行攻击生成和反馈驱动的利用优化，形成闭环搜索，逐步收敛到成功利用。框架动态验证技能漏洞的可利用性，通过对抗性提示进行红队测试。

Result: 在10个LLM上对71个对抗性技能和100个真实世界技能的实验显示，SkillAttack大幅优于所有基线（对抗性技能ASR 0.73-0.93，真实世界技能最高0.26），表明即使在现实智能体交互中，善意技能也构成严重安全风险。

Conclusion: SkillAttack框架揭示了LLM技能生态系统中存在的严重安全风险，即使是非恶意技能也可能被对抗性提示利用，强调了动态漏洞测试的重要性。

Abstract: LLM-based agent systems increasingly rely on agent skills sourced from open registries to extend their capabilities, yet the openness of such ecosystems makes skills difficult to thoroughly vet. Existing attacks rely on injecting malicious instructions into skills, making them easily detectable by static auditing. However, non-malicious skills may also harbor latent vulnerabilities that an attacker can exploit solely through adversarial prompting, without modifying the skill itself. We introduce SkillAttack, a red-teaming framework that dynamically verifies skill vulnerability exploitability through adversarial prompting. SkillAttack combines vulnerability analysis, surface-parallel attack generation, and feedback-driven exploit refinement into a closed-loop search that progressively converges toward successful exploitation. Experiments across 10 LLMs on 71 adversarial and 100 real-world skills show that SkillAttack outperforms all baselines by a wide margin (ASR 0.73--0.93 on adversarial skills, up to 0.26 on real-world skills), revealing that even well-intended skills pose serious security risks under realistic agent interactions.

</details>


### [4] [FreakOut-LLM: The Effect of Emotional Stimuli on Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04992)
*Daniel Kuznetsov,Ofir Cohen,Karin Shistik,Rami Puzis,Asaf Shabtai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究发现情绪刺激（特别是压力情境）会显著降低LLMs的安全对齐效果，使越狱攻击成功率提高65.2%，而放松情境则无影响


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全对齐的LLMs经过拒绝训练来拒绝有害请求，但尚不清楚这些机制在情绪刺激下是否仍然有效。研究旨在探索情绪上下文是否会损害对抗环境中的安全对齐

Method: 提出FreakOut-LLM框架，使用经过验证的心理刺激评估情绪启动如何影响越狱易感性。测试三种条件（压力、放松、中性）加上无提示基线，使用心理学协议场景，在10个LLMs上评估攻击成功率，使用HarmBench在AdvBench提示上评估

Result: 压力启动使越狱成功率比中性条件提高65.2%（z=5.93，p<0.001；OR=1.67，Cohen's d=0.28），放松启动无影响（p=0.84）。10个模型中有5个显示显著脆弱性，最大效应集中在开源权重模型。逻辑回归确认压力是唯一显著的条件预测因子。测量的心理状态强烈预测攻击成功率（|r|≥0.70，所有p<0.001）

Conclusion: 情绪上下文构成可测量的攻击面，对高压力领域中的AI实际部署具有重要影响，表明当前安全对齐机制在情绪刺激下存在脆弱性

Abstract: Safety-aligned LLMs go through refusal training to reject harmful requests, but whether these mechanisms remain effective under emotionally charged stimuli is unexplored. We introduce FreakOut-LLM, a framework investigating whether emotional context compromises safety alignment in adversarial settings. Using validated psychological stimuli, we evaluate how emotional priming through system prompts affects jailbreak susceptibility across ten LLMs. We test three conditions (stress, relaxation, neutral) using scenarios from established psychological protocols, plus a no-prompt baseline, and evaluate attack success using HarmBench on AdvBench prompts. Stress priming increases jailbreak success by 65.2\% compared to neutral conditions (z = 5.93, p < 0.001; OR = 1.67, Cohen's d = 0.28), while relaxation priming produces no effect (p = 0.84). Five of ten models show significant vulnerability, with the largest effects concentrated in open-weight models. Logistic regression on 59,800 queries confirms stress as the sole significant condition predictor after controlling for prompt length (p = 0.61) and model identity. Measured psychological state strongly predicts attack success (|r|\geq0.70 across five instruments; all p < 0.001 in individual-level logistic regression). These results establish emotional context as a measurable attack surface with implications for real-world AI deployment in high-stress domains.

</details>


### [5] [Streaming Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04995)
*Yi Lyu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 设计自适应的区块链区块创建过程，通过数学模型预测交易延迟和成功率，自动选择最优配置以减少交易处理延迟


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链交易存在较长的端到端延迟，其中区块创建步骤是主要瓶颈，需要改善这一性能问题

Method: 1) 定义数学模型预测基于设计和环境参数的交易延迟；2) 开发在docker托管的区块链系统中收集性能指标的测量技术；3) 建立数学模型预测不同密钥访问模式和区块大小配置下的交易成功率；4) 通过仿真测量验证模型

Result: 开发了自适应的区块创建过程，能够根据工作负载和硬件资源自动选择最优配置

Conclusion: 通过数学模型和自适应配置选择，可以有效减少区块链交易延迟，提高系统性能

Abstract: Blockchain and blockchain-inspired decentralized applications are on the rise thanks to their unique characteristics such as their decentralized nature, anonymity, and tamper-proof nature; however, blockchain transactions tend to experience long end-to-end latency, with a major contributor being the block creation step, which might block transaction processing. There are two approaches to ameliorate this overhead: speeding up the block creation process, or processing transactions before block creation finishes. In this project, we work towards designing a self-adaptive block creation process that automatically selects optimal configurations based on workload and hardware resources by defining mathematical models to predict transaction latency based on design and environmental parameters, developing measurement techniques to collect performance-related metrics in docker-hosted blockchain systems and observing trends to build intuition, and defining a mathematical model to predict transaction success rate under various key accessing patterns and block size configurations, validating it with simulation-based measurements.

</details>


### [6] [LanG -- A Governance-Aware Agentic AI Platform for Unified Security Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05440)
*Anes Abdennebi,Nadjia Kara,Laaziz Lahlou,Hakima Ould-Slimane*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LanG是一个开源的、治理感知的智能AI平台，用于统一安全运营，通过LLM辅助实现事件关联、规则生成、攻击重建和治理架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代安全运营中心面临告警疲劳、工具碎片化和跨源事件关联有限的问题，现有SIEM和XDR系统仅通过碎片化工具部分解决这些问题。

Method: 提出LanG平台，包含：统一事件上下文记录与关联引擎、基于LangGraph的智能AI编排器、LLM规则生成器、三阶段攻击重建器，以及分层治理架构（MCP-智能AI-安全架构）。

Result: 关联引擎F1分数87%；规则生成器平均接受率96.2%；攻击重建器杀伤链准确率87.5%；治理管道F1分数98.1%；异常检测加权F1分数99.0%；威胁检测91.0%；推理时间约21ms；平均检测时间1.58秒；规则部署率超过91%。

Conclusion: LanG是首个满足多种工业能力的一体化开源工具，同时强制执行选定的AI治理策略，为托管安全服务提供商提供多租户隔离、基于角色的访问控制和完全本地部署支持。

Abstract: Modern Security Operations Centers struggle with alert fatigue, fragmented tooling, and limited cross-source event correlation. Challenges that current Security Information Event Management and Extended Detection and Response systems only partially address through fragmented tools. This paper presents the LLM-assisted network Governance (LanG), an open-source, governance-aware agentic AI platform for unified security operations contributing: (i) a Unified Incident Context Record with a correlation engine (F1 = 87%), (ii) an Agentic AI Orchestrator on LangGraph with human-in-the-loop checkpoints, (iii) an LLM-based Rule Generator finetuned on four base models producing deployable Snort 2/3, Suricata, and YARA rules (average acceptance rate 96.2%), (iv) a Three-Phase Attack Reconstructor combining Louvain community detection, LLM-driven hypothesis generation, and Bayesian scoring (87.5% kill-chain accuracy), and (v) a layered Governance-MCP-Agentic AI-Security architecture where all tools are exposed via the Model Context Protocol, governed by an AI Governance Policy Engine with a two-layer guardrail pipeline (regex + Llama Prompt Guard 2 semantic classifier, achieving 98.1% F1 score with experimental zero false positives). Designed for Managed Security Service Providers, the platform supports multi-tenant isolation, role-based access, and fully local deployment. Finetuned anomaly and threat detectors achieve weighted F1 scores of 99.0% and 91.0%, respectively, in intrusion-detection benchmarks, running inferences in $\approx$21 ms with a machine-side mean time to detect of 1.58 s, and the rule generator exceeds 91% deployability on live IDS engines. A systematic comparison against eight SOC platforms confirms that LanG uniquely satisfies multiple industrial capabilities all in one open-source tool, while enforcing selected AI governance policies.

</details>


### [7] [AttnDiff: Attention-based Differential Fingerprinting for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05502)
*Haobo Zhang,Zhenhua Xu,Junxian Li,Shangfeng Sheng,Dezhang Kong,Meng Han*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AttnDiff：基于注意力差异的白盒框架，通过分析模型在语义冲突提示对上的信息路由行为来提取指纹，用于验证开源大语言模型的知识产权归属。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保护开源权重大语言模型的知识产权，需要验证可疑模型是否源自受害者模型，即使经过常见的清洗操作（如微调、剪枝/压缩、模型合并等）。现有方法难以应对这些操作带来的模型变化。

Method: 提出AttnDiff框架：1）构建最小编辑的提示对，诱导受控的语义冲突；2）捕获模型在这些提示上的差分注意力模式；3）使用紧凑的谱描述符总结注意力模式；4）通过CKA（中心核对齐）比较模型间的相似性。

Result: 在Llama-2/3、Qwen2.5（3B-14B）及其他开源模型家族上测试，AttnDiff能高精度区分相关衍生模型与无关模型家族（相似度>0.98 vs <0.22，使用M=60个探针）。仅需5-60个多领域探针即可支持实用的来源验证和问责。

Conclusion: AttnDiff通过分析模型内在的信息路由行为，提供了一种数据高效的白盒指纹提取方法，能够有效验证大语言模型的来源，为开源模型的知识产权保护提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Protecting the intellectual property of open-weight large language models (LLMs) requires verifying whether a suspect model is derived from a victim model despite common laundering operations such as fine-tuning (including PPO/DPO), pruning/compression, and model merging. We propose \textsc{AttnDiff}, a data-efficient white-box framework that extracts fingerprints from models via intrinsic information-routing behavior. \textsc{AttnDiff} probes minimally edited prompt pairs that induce controlled semantic conflicts, captures differential attention patterns, summarizes them with compact spectral descriptors, and compares models using CKA. Across Llama-2/3 and Qwen2.5 (3B--14B) and additional open-source families, it yields high similarity for related derivatives while separating unrelated model families (e.g., $>0.98$ vs.\ $<0.22$ with $M=60$ probes). With 5--60 multi-domain probes, it supports practical provenance verification and accountability.

</details>


### [8] [Understanding User Privacy Perceptions of GenAI Smartphones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05571)
*Ran Jin,Liu Wang,Shidong Pan,Luona Xu,Tianming Liu,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 用户对GenAI智能手机的隐私担忧与设计期望研究：通过访谈发现用户对系统运作理解有限，但了解技术细节后隐私担忧加剧，提出跨系统控制、数据管理和透明度的隐私增强建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI智能手机通过系统级嵌入生成式AI，能够自动化任务并代表用户执行UI操作，但其强大功能依赖于持续访问敏感和上下文丰富的数据，引发了超越传统移动设备的隐私担忧。目前用户对这些设备的隐私影响感知和期望的安全措施了解甚少，在GenAI智能手机早期采用阶段这一问题尤为关键。

Method: 研究采用定性方法，首先对22名日常移动用户进行半结构化访谈，探索他们对GenAI智能手机的使用情况、隐私担忧和隐私设计期望。随后进行后续焦点小组讨论，让参与者讨论隐私增强建议。

Result: 研究发现：1) 用户在使用GenAI智能手机时对其系统运作机制理解有限；2) 一旦暴露于技术细节，用户的隐私担忧显著加剧；3) 参与者的担忧涵盖整个数据生命周期，包括不透明的收集、不安全的存储和薄弱的数据控制；4) 焦点小组提出了跨系统级控制、数据管理实践和用户透明度的协调隐私增强建议。

Conclusion: 用户对GenAI智能手机的担忧和建议为设计平衡功能性与隐私保护的设备提供了以用户为中心的指导。这些发现对系统设计者和监管者具有重要价值，强调了在GenAI智能手机设计中需要协调系统级控制、数据管理和透明度机制，以建立用户信任并促进负责任的技术采用。

Abstract: GenAI smartphones, which natively embed generative AI at the system level, are transforming mobile interactions by automating a wide range of tasks and executing UI actions on behalf of users. Their superior capabilities rely on continuous access to sensitive and context-rich data, raising privacy concerns that surpass those of traditional mobile devices. Yet, little is known about how users perceive the privacy implications of such devices or what safeguards they expect, which is especially critical at this early stage of GenAI smartphone adoption. To address this gap, we conduct 22 semi-structured interviews with everyday mobile users to explore their usage of GenAI smartphones, privacy concerns, and privacy design expectations. Our findings show that users engage with GenAI smartphones with limited understanding of how these systems operate to deliver functions, but show heightened privacy concerns once exposed to the technical details. Participants' concerns span the entire data lifecycle, including nontransparent collection, insecure storage, and weak data control. In a follow-up focus group, participants discuss a range of privacy-enhancing suggestions that call for coordinated changes across system-level controls, data management practices, and user-facing transparency. Their concerns and suggestions offer user-centered guidances for designing GenAI smartphones that balance functionality with privacy protection, offering valuable takeaways for system designers and regulators.

</details>


### [9] [T2T: Captioning Smartphone Activities Using Mobile Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05642)
*Jiyu Liu,Yong Huang,Yanzhao Lu,Yun Tie,Wanqing Tu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: T2T系统通过加密移动流量生成智能手机活动文本描述，解决了流量特征与文本描述间的语义鸿沟和缺乏文本标注数据的问题，在真实环境中实现了高质量的活动描述生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统智能手机活动分类方法在模型可扩展性和输出可读性方面存在局限，且存在两个主要障碍：流量特征与活动描述之间的语义鸿沟，以及缺乏文本标注的流量数据。

Method: 提出T2T系统，包含流量特征编码器（将低级流量特征转换为潜在特征）和描述解码器（生成可读的活动描述）。通过Qwen-VL-Max视觉语言模型自动标注流量数据，并使用多阶段损失进行跨模型训练。

Result: 在2个真实环境、8名用户、20个应用的40,000个流量-描述对上评估，T2T获得BLEU-4:58.1、METEOR:38.3、ROUGE-L:70.5、CIDEr:108.7的高分，生成质量与视觉语言模型相当。

Conclusion: T2T系统成功解决了移动流量活动描述生成的关键挑战，能够生成语义准确的文本描述，为智能手机活动监控和理解提供了新的有效方法。

Abstract: This paper studies the creation of textual descriptions of user activities and interactions on smartphones. Our approach of referring to encrypted mobile traffic exceeds traditional smartphone activity classification methods in terms of model scalability and output readability. The paper addresses two obstacles to the realization of this idea: the semantic gap between traffic features and smartphone activity captions, and the lack of textually annotated traffic data. To overcome these challenges, we introduce a novel smartphone activity captioning system, called T2T (Traffic-to-Text). T2T consists of a flow feature encoder that converts low-level traffic characteristics into meaningful latent features and a caption decoder to yield readable transcripts of smartphone activities. In addition, T2T achieves the automatic textual annotation of mobile traffic by feeding synchronized screen capture videos into the Qwen-VL-Max vision-language model, and proposing multi-stage losses for effective cross-model training. We evaluate T2T on 40,000 traffic-description pairs collected in two real-world environments, involving 8 smartphone users and 20 mobile apps. T2T achieves a BLEU-4 score of 58.1, a METEOR score of 38.3, a ROUGE-L score of 70.5, and a CIDEr score of 108.7. The quantitative and qualitative analyses show that T2T can generate semantically accurate captions that are comparable to the vision-language model.

</details>


### [10] [BodhiPromptShield: Pre-Inference Prompt Mediation for Suppressing Privacy Propagation in LLM/VLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05793)
*Bo Ma,Jinsong Wu,Weiqi Yan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BodhiPromptShield是一个针对LLM/VLM智能体的提示隐私保护框架，通过检测敏感信息、使用占位符/语义抽象/安全符号映射进行路由，并在授权边界延迟恢复，以解决敏感信息在检索、记忆、工具调用等阶段传播的隐私风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM/VLM智能体中，提示隐私风险会跨越单个模型调用传播，原始用户内容可能流入检索查询、记忆写入、工具调用和日志中。现有的去标识化流程只处理文档边界，无法解决这种跨阶段传播问题。

Method: 提出BodhiPromptShield框架：1)检测敏感信息片段；2)通过类型化占位符、语义抽象或安全符号映射进行路由；3)在授权边界延迟恢复敏感信息。该框架增加了显式的传播感知中介和恢复时机作为安全变量。

Result: 在受控的提示隐私基准测试(CPPB)上评估：阶段传播抑制从10.7%降至7.1%(跨检索、记忆和工具阶段)；PER达到9.3%，AC为0.94，TSR为0.92，优于通用去标识化方法。这些是基于CPPB的受控系统结果，而非正式隐私保证或公共基准迁移声明。

Conclusion: BodhiPromptShield通过政策感知的敏感信息管理框架，有效解决了LLM/VLM智能体中提示隐私的跨阶段传播问题，在保持功能性的同时提供了更好的隐私保护。

Abstract: In LLM/VLM agents, prompt privacy risk propagates beyond a single model call because raw user content can flow into retrieval queries, memory writes, tool calls, and logs. Existing de-identification pipelines address document boundaries but not this cross-stage propagation. We propose BodhiPromptShield, a policy-aware framework that detects sensitive spans, routes them via typed placeholders, semantic abstraction, or secure symbolic mapping, and delays restoration to authorized boundaries. Relative to enterprise redaction, this adds explicit propagation-aware mediation and restoration timing as a security variable. Under controlled evaluation on the Controlled Prompt-Privacy Benchmark (CPPB), stage-wise propagation suppresses from 10.7\% to 7.1\% across retrieval, memory, and tool stages; PER reaches 9.3\% with 0.94 AC and 0.92 TSR, outperforming generic de-identification. These are controlled systems results on CPPB rather than formal privacy guarantees or public-benchmark transfer claims.
  The project repository is available at https://github.com/mabo1215/BodhiPromptShield.git.

</details>


### [11] [Stealthy and Adjustable Text-Guided Backdoor Attacks on Multimodal Pretrained Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05809)
*Yiyang Zhang,Chaojian Yu,Ziming Hong,Yuanjie Shao,Qinmu Peng,Tongliang Liu,Xinge You*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出文本引导后门攻击方法，利用文本描述中的常见词作为后门触发器，结合视觉对抗扰动实现可控攻击，在CIR和VQA任务中验证了实用性和隐蔽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态预训练模型后门攻击主要依赖视觉或多模态触发器，这些触发器在真实数据中很少出现，缺乏实用性和隐蔽性。需要开发更隐蔽、实用的攻击方法。

Method: 提出文本引导后门攻击：1) 使用文本描述中的常见词作为后门触发器；2) 在中毒样本上引入视觉对抗扰动，调节模型对文本触发器的学习；3) 实现可控可调节的攻击。

Result: 在基于多模态预训练模型的下游任务（CIR和VQA）上进行了广泛实验，TGB攻击在多种现实场景下实现了实用性和隐蔽性，攻击成功率可调节，揭示了多模态预训练模型的安全漏洞。

Conclusion: 文本引导后门攻击通过使用常见文本词作为触发器，显著提高了攻击的隐蔽性和实用性，结合视觉对抗扰动实现了可控攻击，揭示了多模态预训练模型的关键安全漏洞。

Abstract: Multimodal pretrained models are vulnerable to backdoor attacks, yet most existing methods rely on visual or multimodal triggers, which are impractical since visually embedded triggers rarely occur in real-world data. To overcome this limitation, we propose a novel Text-Guided Backdoor (TGB) attack on multimodal pretrained models, where commonly occurring words in textual descriptions serve as backdoor triggers, significantly improving stealthiness and practicality. Furthermore, we introduce visual adversarial perturbations on poisoned samples to modulate the model's learning of textual triggers, enabling a controllable and adjustable TGB attack. Extensive experiments on downstream tasks built upon multimodal pretrained models, including Composed Image Retrieval (CIR) and Visual Question Answering (VQA), demonstrate that TGB achieves practicality and stealthiness with adjustable attack success rates across diverse realistic settings, revealing critical security vulnerabilities in multimodal pretrained models.

</details>


### [12] [Inertial Mining: Equilibrium Implementation of the Bitcoin Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06092)
*Manuel Mueller-Frank,Minghao Pan,Omer Tamuz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种名为"惯性挖矿"的新协议，解决比特币自私挖矿问题，使诚实挖矿成为均衡策略


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特币挖矿协议存在激励问题，自私挖矿等偏离策略可以获利，破坏了协议的均衡性，需要设计一个能确保诚实行为成为均衡的新协议

Method: 提出惯性挖矿协议，该协议仅在发生分叉时改变矿工行为，不需要修改比特币共识机制或区块链架构，确保矿工在发现新区块时立即广播

Result: 惯性挖矿使诚实挖矿成为均衡策略（假设没有矿工控制超过一半算力），自私挖矿等偏离策略不再有利可图，且能产生单一最长链

Conclusion: 惯性挖矿协议解决了比特币的自私挖矿问题，使诚实行为成为均衡，同时保持与现有比特币系统的兼容性

Abstract: The value of proof-of-work cryptocurrencies critically depends on miners having incentives to follow the protocol. However, the Bitcoin mining protocol proposed by Nakamoto (2008) and implemented in practice is well known not to constitute an equilibrium: Eyal and Sirer (2018) construct a profitable deviation called ``selfish mining'' which relies on strategically delaying disclosure of newly mined blocks rather than publishing them immediately. We propose inertial mining, a novel mining protocol. When miners follow inertial mining, they produce the outcome intended by Nakamoto, i.e., a single longest chain. But unlike the Bitcoin mining protocol, inertial mining constitutes an equilibrium (assuming no miner controls more than half of the mining power). Indeed, neither selfish mining nor any other deviation is profitable. Furthermore, inertial mining only changes miners' behavior in the event of off-path forks, and can be implemented in Bitcoin without any changes to its consensus mechanism or blockchain architecture.

</details>


### [13] [LLM4CodeRE: Generative AI for Code Decompilation Analysis and Reverse Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06095)
*Hamed Jelodar,Samita Bai,Tochukwu Emmanuel Nwankwo,Parisa Hamedi,Mohammad Meymani,Roozbeh Razavi-Far,Ali A. Ghorbani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LLM4CodeRE：一个用于双向代码逆向工程的领域自适应LLM框架，支持汇编到源代码的反编译和源代码到汇编的翻译


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶意软件逆向工程中的代码反编译分析面临挑战，主要由于广泛使用的复杂混淆技术。现有的大语言模型方法主要依赖通用代码预训练，缺乏对恶意软件的适应性。

Method: 提出LLM4CodeRE框架，采用两种互补的微调策略：(1) 多适配器方法用于任务特定的语法和语义对齐；(2) 序列到序列统一方法使用任务条件前缀来强制执行端到端生成约束。

Result: 实验结果表明，LLM4CodeRE优于现有的反编译工具和通用代码模型，实现了鲁棒的双向泛化能力。

Conclusion: LLM4CodeRE是一个有效的领域自适应LLM框架，能够在恶意软件逆向工程中实现高质量的双向代码转换，为安全分析提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Code decompilation analysis is a fundamental yet challenging task in malware reverse engineering, particularly due to the pervasive use of sophisticated obfuscation techniques. Although recent large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in translating low-level representations into high-level source code, most existing approaches rely on generic code pretraining and lack adaptation to malicious software. We propose LLM4CodeRE, a domain-adaptive LLM framework for bidirectional code reverse engineering that supports both assembly-to-source decompilation and source-to-assembly translation within a unified model. To enable effective task adaptation, we introduce two complementary fine-tuning strategies: (i) a Multi-Adapter approach for task-specific syntactic and semantic alignment, and (ii) a Seq2Seq Unified approach using task-conditioned prefixes to enforce end-to-end generation constraints. Experimental results demonstrate that LLM4CodeRE outperforms existing decompilation tools and general-purpose code models, achieving robust bidirectional generalization.

</details>


### [14] [Signature Placement in Post-Quantum TLS Certificate Hierarchies: An Experimental Study of ML-DSA and SLH-DSA in TLS 1.3 Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06100)
*José Luis Delgado Jiménez*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TLS 1.3后量子迁移不是简单的签名算法替换问题，而是证书层次设计、链暴露和认证期间密码成本集中的系统性问题。研究发现SLH-DSA放在服务器叶证书时会导致握手延迟和服务器计算成本数量级增加，而将其限制在上层信任层可保持合理操作范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TLS 1.3后量子迁移不应被视为简单的签名算法替换问题，而需要考虑签名家族在证书层次中的位置、握手期间暴露的层次范围以及密码负担在客户端和服务器角色间的分布。现有方法过于依赖原始级基准测试来推断部署成本，忽略了实际认证策略的系统性影响。

Method: 基于OpenSSL 3和oqsprovider构建TLS 1.3认证策略的本地实验研究。使用可重现实验室，比较ML-DSA和SLH-DSA在不同证书放置位置、层次深度和密钥交换模式下的性能，包括经典、混合和纯后量子配置。

Result: 最显著的性能断点出现在SLH-DSA放置在服务器叶证书时：握手延迟和服务器端计算成本增加数个数量级。将SLH-DSA限制在上层信任层并保持ML-DSA在交互叶证书的策略保持在更合理的操作范围内。传输大小本身不能解释重负载机制，一旦SLH-DSA到达叶证书，服务器端密码成本成为主导因素。

Conclusion: 后量子TLS迁移应作为证书层次设计、链暴露和实时认证期间密码成本集中的问题进行评估。策略设计需要考虑签名算法在证书层次中的位置分布，避免将计算密集型算法放在交互叶证书，以平衡安全性和性能。

Abstract: Post-quantum migration in TLS 1.3 should not be understood as a flat substitution problem in which one signature algorithm is replaced by another and deployment cost is inferred directly from primitive-level benchmarks. In certificate-based authentication, the practical effect of a signature family depends on where it appears in the certification hierarchy, how much of that hierarchy is exposed during the handshake, and how cryptographic burden is distributed across client and server roles.
  This paper presents a local experimental study of TLS 1.3 authentication strategies built on OpenSSL 3 and oqsprovider. Using a reproducible laboratory, it compares ML-DSA and SLH-DSA across multiple certificate placements, hierarchy depths, and key-exchange modes, including classical, hybrid, and pure post-quantum configurations.
  The clearest discontinuity appears when SLH-DSA is placed in the server leaf certificate. In that configuration, handshake latency and server-side compute cost increase by orders of magnitude, while strategies that confine SLH-DSA to upper trust layers and preserve ML-DSA in the interactive leaf remain within a substantially more plausible operational range. The results further show that transport size alone does not explain the heavy regime: once SLH-DSA reaches the leaf, server-side cryptographic cost becomes dominant.
  The paper argues that post-quantum TLS migration is best evaluated as a problem of certificate-hierarchy design, chain exposure, and cryptographic cost concentration during live authentication.

</details>

### [15] [GLANCE: A Global-Local Coordination Multi-Agent Framework for Music-Grounded Non-Linear Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05076)
*Zihao Lin,Haibo Wang,Zhiyang Xu,Siyao Dai,Huanjie Dong,Xiaohan Wang,Yolo Y. Tang,Yixin Wang,Qifan Wang,Lifu Huang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: GLANCE是一个用于音乐驱动的混搭视频创作的多智能体框架，采用双层循环架构和全局-局部协调机制，在MVEBench基准测试中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音乐驱动的混搭视频创作方法通常依赖固定流程或简化的检索-拼接范式，难以适应多样化的提示和异质源材料，需要更灵活、协调的编辑框架。

Method: GLANCE采用双层循环架构：外层循环进行长时程规划和任务图构建，内层循环采用"观察-思考-行动-验证"流程进行分段编辑任务和精炼。引入全局-局部协调机制，包括上下文控制器、冲突区域分解模块和自底向上的动态协商机制。

Result: 在MVEBench基准测试中，使用GPT-4o-mini作为骨干模型时，GLANCE在两个任务设置上分别比最强基线提升33.2%和15.6%。人工评估进一步证实了生成视频的质量和评估框架的有效性。

Conclusion: GLANCE通过多智能体框架和全局-局部协调机制，显著提升了音乐驱动混搭视频创作的质量和适应性，为非线性视频编辑提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Music-grounded mashup video creation is a challenging form of video non-linear editing, where a system must compose a coherent timeline from large collections of source videos while aligning with music rhythm, user intent, story completeness, and long-range structural constraints. Existing approaches typically rely on fixed pipelines or simplified retrieval-and-concatenation paradigms, limiting their ability to adapt to diverse prompts and heterogeneous source materials. In this paper, we present GLANCE, a global-local coordination multi-agent framework for music-grounded nonlinear video editing. GLANCE adopts a bi-loop architecture for better editing practice: an outer loop performs long-horizon planning and task-graph construction, and an inner loop adopts the "Observe-Think-Act-Verify" flow for segment-wise editing tasks and their refinements. To address the cross-segment and global conflict emerging after subtimelines composition, we introduce a dedicated global-local coordination mechanism with both preventive and corrective components, which includes a novelly designed context controller, conflict region decomposition module, and a bottom-up dynamic negotiation mechanism. To support rigorous evaluation, we construct MVEBench, a new benchmark that factorizes editing difficulty along task type, prompt specificity, and music length, and propose an agent-as-a-judge evaluation framework for scalable multi-dimensional assessment. Experimental results show that GLANCE consistently outperforms prior research baselines and open-source product baselines under the same backbone models. With GPT-4o-mini as the backbone, GLANCE improves over the strongest baseline by 33.2% and 15.6% on two task settings, respectively. Human evaluation further confirms the quality of the generated videos and validates the effectiveness of the proposed evaluation framework.

</details>


### [16] [Nash Approximation Gap in Truncated Infinite-horizon Partially Observable Markov Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05131)
*Lan Sang,Chinmay Maheshwari*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出有限记忆截断框架，将无限时域部分可观测马尔可夫博弈近似为有限状态、有限行动的马尔可夫博弈，通过截断信息窗口实现计算可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观测马尔可夫博弈（POMGs）为不对称信息下的多智能体序贯决策提供通用框架，但将其转化为信念状态上的完全可观测马尔可夫博弈在无限时域下不可行，因为信念状态和行动空间会随时间信息积累而增长。

Method: 提出有限记忆截断框架：将无限时域POMGs近似为有限状态、有限行动的马尔可夫博弈，智能体仅基于有限窗口的公共和私有信息做出决策，而非完整历史信息。

Result: 在适当的滤波器稳定性（遗忘）条件下，证明截断博弈的任何纳什均衡都是原始POMG的ε-纳什均衡，且当截断长度增加时ε趋近于0。

Conclusion: 有限记忆截断框架为无限时域POMGs提供了计算可行的近似方法，通过有限信息窗口实现有效决策，在滤波器稳定性条件下保证近似均衡的质量。

Abstract: Partially Observable Markov Games (POMGs) provide a general framework for modeling multi-agent sequential decision-making under asymmetric information. A common approach is to reformulate a POMG as a fully observable Markov game over belief states, where the state is the conditional distribution of the system state and agents' private information given common information, and actions correspond to mappings (prescriptions) from private information to actions. However, this reformulation is intractable in infinite-horizon settings, as both the belief state and action spaces grow with the accumulation of information over time. We propose a finite-memory truncation framework that approximates infinite-horizon POMGs by a finite-state, finite-action Markov game, where agents condition decisions only on finite windows of common and private information. Under suitable filter stability (forgetting) conditions, we show that any Nash equilibrium of the truncated game is an $\varepsilon$-Nash equilibrium of the original POMG, where $\varepsilon \to 0$ as the truncation length increases.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [17] [Probabilistic Tree Inference Enabled by FDSOI Ferroelectric FETs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05115)
*Pengyu Ren,Xingtian Wang,Boyang Cheng,Jiahui Duan,Giuk Kim,Xuezhong Niu,Halid Mulaosmanovic,Stefan Duenkel,Sven Beyer,X. Sharon Hu,Ningyuan Cao,Kai Ni*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 提出基于FDSOI-FeFET的单片硬件平台，原生支持ACAM和GRNG功能，用于高效实现贝叶斯决策树，在不确定性量化、可解释性和噪声鲁棒性方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶、医疗诊断和金融系统等应用需要具备不确定性量化、可解释性和噪声鲁棒性的机器学习模型。现有贝叶斯决策树硬件实现存在内存瓶颈、处理模式不规则、多平台集成复杂和能耗高等问题。

Method: 开发单片FDSOI-FeFET硬件平台，利用铁电晶体管极化实现紧凑的多位存储用于ACAM，利用栅漏重叠区带间隧穿和空穴存储提供高质量熵源用于GRNG，原生支持ACAM和GRNG功能。

Result: 在MNIST数据集上，相比传统决策树，在数据集噪声和设备变化下分类准确率提升超过40%；相比CPU和GPU基线，速度提升超过两个数量级，能效提升超过四个数量级。

Conclusion: 该单片FDSOI-FeFET平台为贝叶斯决策树提供了可扩展的硬件解决方案，在资源受限和安全关键环境中具有显著优势，实现了高效的不确定性估计、可解释性和噪声容忍度。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence applications in autonomous driving, medical diagnostics, and financial systems increasingly demand machine learning models that can provide robust uncertainty quantification, interpretability, and noise resilience. Bayesian decision trees (BDTs) are attractive for these tasks because they combine probabilistic reasoning, interpretable decision-making, and robustness to noise. However, existing hardware implementations of BDTs based on CPUs and GPUs are limited by memory bottlenecks and irregular processing patterns, while multi-platform solutions exploiting analog content-addressable memory (ACAM) and Gaussian random number generators (GRNGs) introduce integration complexity and energy overheads. Here we report a monolithic FDSOI-FeFET hardware platform that natively supports both ACAM and GRNG functionalities. The ferroelectric polarization of FeFETs enables compact, energy-efficient multi-bit storage for ACAM, and band-to-band tunneling in the gate-to-drain overlap region and subsequent hole storage in the floating body provides a high-quality entropy source for GRNG. System-level evaluations demonstrate that the proposed architecture provides robust uncertainty estimation, interpretability, and noise tolerance with high energy efficiency. Under both dataset noise and device variations, it achieves over 40% higher classification accuracy on MNIST compared to conventional decision trees. Moreover, it delivers more than two orders of magnitude speedup over CPU and GPU baselines and over four orders of magnitude improvement in energy efficiency, making it a scalable solution for deploying BDTs in resource-constrained and safety-critical environments.

</details>


### [18] [Experimental Demonstration of an On-Chip CMOS-Integrated 3T-1MTJ Probabilistic Bit - A P-Bit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05191)
*Xuejian Zhang,John Arnesh Divakaruni Daniel,Neil Dilley,Zhihong Chen,Joerg Appenzeller*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 首次实验演示了完全CMOS集成的基于随机磁性隧道结的随机比特，仅需3个晶体管+1个随机磁性隧道结即可产生轨到轨随机输出，为大规模概率计算架构铺平道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体缩放面临挑战，神经形态计算兴起，需要探索新型计算方案以实现更高能效和计算能力。概率计算具有低功耗、能解决概率编码问题、易于与现有CMOS技术集成等优势。

Method: 开发了基于随机磁性隧道结的随机比特，采用完全CMOS集成设计，仅需3个晶体管+1个随机磁性隧道结。通过实验演示该随机比特的功能，并通过仿真验证其在概率逻辑电路中的性能。

Result: 成功实现了首个完全CMOS集成的随机磁性隧道结基随机比特，能够产生轨到轨随机输出。仿真确认该随机比特在概率逻辑电路中功能正常。

Conclusion: 该随机比特的演示为实现单片大规模概率计算架构在CMOS芯片上的实现铺平了道路，为克服半导体缩放挑战和推进神经形态计算提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Ongoing semiconductor scaling challenges and the rise of neuromorphic computing have sparked interest in exploring novel computing schemes to achieve higher power efficiency and computational capabilities. Probabilistic computing is one candidate that endows low power consumption, capability of solving probability-encoded computational problems, and the ease of integration with existing CMOS technology. A basic building block of this scheme is the probabilistic bit (P-Bit), which utilizes a novel device such as a stochastic magnetic tunnel junction (sMTJ) to generate tunable randomness by nature. This work presents the first experimental demonstration of a fully CMOS-integrated sMTJ-based P-Bit, capable of generating rail-to-rail stochastic output with a mere collection of 3 transistors + 1 sMTJ. Furthermore, simulations also confirm this P-Bit's functionality in probabilistic logic circuits. The demonstration of such P-Bit paves the way towards realizing monolithic large-scale probabilistic computing architecture on CMOS chips.

</details>


### [19] [Late Breaking Results: Hardware-Efficient Quantum Reservoir Computing via Quantized Readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06075)
*Param Pathak,Mansi Od,Nouhaila Innan,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 提出基于固定量子电路的硬件高效量子储层计算框架，采用切比雪夫特征编码和遗传搜索优化架构，通过8位/6位定点量化在保持精度的同时显著减少内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着电力需求增长，短期负荷预测对电网稳定性和能源管理日益重要，特别是在资源受限的边缘计算场景中，需要硬件高效的解决方案。

Method: 采用固定、未经训练的量子电路作为储层，包含切比雪夫特征编码、砖块式纠缠结构以及单/双量子比特泡利测量，避免量子反向传播；通过遗传算法从18种候选配置中选择最优架构；对经典读出层进行训练后定点量化。

Result: 在有限测量次数评估下，8位和6位量化分别将读出层内存减少75%和81%，同时保持预测精度在FP32基线的1%范围内；使用Tetouan城市电力消耗数据集验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 量化读出层可以显著提高量子储层计算的硬件效率和部署实用性，为内存受限的边缘能源预测应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Due to rising electricity demand, accurate short-term load forecasting is increasingly important for grid stability and efficient energy management, particularly in resource-constrained edge settings. We present a hardware-efficient Quantum Reservoir Computing (QRC) framework based on a fixed, untrained quantum circuit with Chebyshev feature encoding, brickwork entanglement, and single- and two-qubit Pauli measurements, avoiding quantum backpropagation entirely. Using the Tetouan City Power Consumption dataset, we examine the effect of post-training fixed-point quantization on the classical readout layer, with the reservoir architecture selected through a genetic search over 18 candidate configurations. Under finite-shot evaluation, 8-bit and 6-bit quantization maintain forecasting accuracy within 1% of the FP32 baseline while reducing readout memory by 75% and 81%, respectively. These results suggest that quantized readout can improve the hardware efficiency and deployment practicality of QRC for memory-constrained energy forecasting.

</details>

### [20] [From Use to Oversight: How Mental Models Influence User Behavior and Output in AI Writing Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05166)
*Shalaleh Rismani,Su Lin Blodgett,Q. Vera Liao,Alexandra Olteanu,AJung Moon*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨用户对AI写作助手的心智模型如何影响其控制行为和写作结果，发现结构心智模型虽提升系统理解，却可能导致更多语法错误


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI写作助手已普及，但用户心智模型如何影响其使用行为尚不清楚。研究旨在探索功能性和结构性两种心智模型如何影响用户对AI建议的控制行为（请求、接受、编辑）和写作结果

Method: 通过不同系统描述诱导48名参与者形成功能性或结构性心智模型，然后使用写作助手完成求职信任务。助手偶尔提供预设的语法错误建议，以测试心智模型是否影响用户的批判性监督

Result: 结构性心智模型参与者对系统理解更好，但出现反效果：他们评价系统更易用，却产生了更多语法错误的信件。这表明在需要用户监督易错AI输出的情境中，系统理解、信任和控制之间存在复杂关系

Conclusion: 用户对AI系统的心智模型显著影响其使用行为和结果。结构性心智模型虽增强系统理解，但可能降低对AI建议的批判性监督，导致更多错误。这揭示了在AI辅助写作中，系统透明度与用户监督之间的微妙平衡

Abstract: AI-based writing assistants are ubiquitous, yet little is known about how users' mental models shape their use. We examine two types of mental models -- functional or related to what the system does, and structural or related to how the system works -- and how they affect control behavior -- how users request, accept, or edit AI suggestions as they write -- and writing outcomes. We primed participants ($N = 48$) with different system descriptions to induce these mental models before asking them to complete a cover letter writing task using a writing assistant that occasionally offered preconfigured ungrammatical suggestions to test whether the mental models affected participants' critical oversight. We find that while participants in the structural mental model condition demonstrate a better understanding of the system, this can have a backfiring effect: while these participants judged the system as more usable, they also produced letters with more grammatical errors, highlighting a complex relationship between system understanding, trust, and control in contexts that require user oversight of error-prone AI outputs.

</details>


### [21] [Investigating Ethical Data Communication with Purrsuasion: An Educational Game about Negotiated Data Disclosure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05200)
*Krisha Mehta,Sami Elahi,Alex Kale*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Purrsuasion是一个开源可视化游戏，用于研究数据通信中的伦理困境，特别是涉及披露约束时数据提供者和寻求者之间的协商动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据通信中存在伦理困境，当情境约束禁止完全披露源数据时，传统可视化研究常将伦理问题简化为个体欺骗设计或被误导，而实际披露问题涉及亲社会行为者之间的协商。需要观察这些情境判断的机制。

Method: 开发Purrsuasion开源可视化游戏，参与者扮演数据提供者（在披露约束下设计可视化）和数据寻求者（请求信息并授予合同）。在本科数据科学课程（N=27）中部署，收集游戏数据支持混合方法分析学生的沟通动态、问题解决和信任形成。

Result: 研究发现，难以设想理想可视化解决方案导致可视化创作中的满意化（satisficing）和难以归因作者意图。基于这些挑战，开发启发式评分标准来支持披露遵守的社会技术判断。

Conclusion: Purrsuasion游戏为研究数据通信伦理困境提供了观察工具，揭示了在披露约束下可视化创作和解释的复杂性，需要社会技术方法评估伦理合规性。

Abstract: Data communication entails ethical dilemmas where situational constraints forbid full disclosure of source data. Whereas visualization research and pedagogy often frames ethics as a matter of individuals making deceptive design choices or being misled, disclosure problems involve negotiation between pro-social actors. To provide observability into these situated judgments, we contribute Purrsuasion, an open-source visualization game where participants play the roles of (i) data providers designing visualizations subject to disclosure constraints and (ii) data seekers requesting information and awarding a contract. We deploy Purrsuasion in an undergraduate data science class (N = 27), gathering gameplay data to support a mixed-methods analysis of students' communication dynamics, problem solving, and trust formation. We find that difficulties envisioning an ideal visualization solution lead to satisficing in visualization authoring and difficulties attributing authorial intent. Given these challenges, we approach scoring student solutions by developing a heuristic rubric that supports sociotechnical judgments of disclosure adherence.

</details>


### [22] [Understanding Clinician Experiences with Game-Based Interventions for Autistic Children to Inform a Future Game Platform Focused on Improving Motor Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05249)
*Hunter M Beach,Devin Jay D San Nicolas,Carly Miller,Cathy Ly,Jared Duval*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 开发模块化平台AutMotion Studio，通过可定制小游戏解决自闭症儿童运动干预严肃游戏的僵化问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自闭症儿童普遍存在运动挑战，游戏能提供临床意义结果和激励环境，但现有解决方案过于僵化，缺乏灵活性

Method: 两阶段定性研究：半结构化访谈和参与式设计工作坊，涉及7名物理治疗师和5名职业治疗师；通过主题分析识别8个关键主题

Result: 识别出当前成功干预的关键特征、机会和临床实践中的障碍；提出模块化平台AutMotion Studio设计，支持可定制小游戏和社区贡献

Conclusion: AutMotion Studio通过模块化设计、社区贡献和Wizard of Oz范式解决了严肃健康游戏的僵化问题，为自闭症儿童运动干预提供了灵活平台

Abstract: Motor challenges are prevalent among autistic children, and games are able to simultaneously produce clinically meaningful results and provide a motivating context, but many current solutions are too rigid. We conducted a two-phase qualitative study comprised of semi-structured interviews and participatory design workshops with 7 pediatric physical and 5 occupational therapists (PTs/OTs) to investigate their perspectives and experiences with game and play-based interventions. We identified 8 prominent themes describing key characteristics of current successful interventions, opportunities, and barriers to adoption in clinical practice. We present a speculative design informed by thematic analysis that addresses current challenges of rigidity in Serious Games for Health (SG4H). Our modular platform (AutMotion Studio) hosts a suite of interventions as customizable minigames, allowing community members to contribute to and employ Wizard of Oz paradigms for flexible appropriation strategies.

</details>


### [23] [Semantic Reality: Interactive Context-Aware Visualization of Inter-Object Relationships in Augmented Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05265)
*Xiaoan Liu,Eric J Gonzalez,Nels Numan,Andrea Colaço,Lucy Abramyan,Chen Zhu-Tian,Ryo Suzuki,Mar Gonzalez-Franco*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Semantic Reality是一个增强现实系统，通过多模态推理、空间锚定和物理动作识别，构建并可视化物体间的连接关系，支持多物体交互任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AR系统主要针对单个物体，难以支持需要理解多个物体间关系的规划、比较和组装任务。物理世界与数字世界的交互仍面临核心挑战。

Method: 提出连接性中心的交互范式，结合锚点跟踪、动作感知和模型推理构建实时连接图。利用多模态推理、空间锚定和物理动作识别维护物体及其关系的持久模型。

Result: 探索性研究表明，与单物体基线相比，参与者报告了更清晰的物体间理解、更高的参与度和满意度，且没有增加工作负荷。场景研究展示了连接性在规划、排序和消歧方面的帮助。

Conclusion: Semantic Reality通过可视化物体间连接关系，增强了AR系统中多物体交互能力，为规划、比较和组装任务提供了更好的支持。

Abstract: Bridging the physical and digital world through interaction remains a core challenge in augmented reality (AR). Existing systems target single objects, limiting support for planning, comparison, and assembly tasks that depend on relationships among multiple items. We present Semantic Reality, an AR system focused on surfacing inter-object connectivity and making it interactive. Leveraging multimodal reasoning, spatial anchoring, and physical action recognition, Semantic Reality maintains a persistent model of objects around the user and their relationships. Connections are visualized in-situ to highlight compatibility, reveal next steps, and reduce ambiguity during tasks. We contribute a connectivity-centered interaction paradigm and a system architecture that couples anchor tracking, action sensing, and model inference to construct a live connectivity graph. In an exploratory study comparing Semantic Reality to a single-object baseline, participants reported clearer inter-object understanding and higher engagement and satisfaction, without increased workload. A scenario study illustrates where connectivity aids planning, sequencing, and disambiguation.

</details>


### [24] [Symetra: Visual Analytics for the Parameter Tuning Process of Symbolic Execution Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05349)
*Donghee Hong,Minjong Kim,Sooyoung Cha,Jaemin Jo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Symetra：一个用于符号执行引擎参数调优的可视化分析系统，支持人机协同优化分支覆盖率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号执行引擎（如KLEE）有众多参数影响分支覆盖率，但参数影响难以理解，用户依赖次优默认配置。自动调优器效果有限且无法解释配置为何有效，需要人机协同方法。

Method: 开发Symetra可视化分析系统，提供两种互补的参数配置影响概览视图，支持集体分析以对比配置组并识别影响分支覆盖率的差异。

Result: 专家不仅能解释参数影响并识别互补配置，而且在分支覆盖率和调优效率方面都优于完全自动化的方法。

Conclusion: Symetra系统有效支持人机协同参数调优，通过可视化分析帮助专家理解参数影响并改进配置，超越了纯自动化方法的性能。

Abstract: Symbolic execution engines such as KLEE automatically generate test cases to maximize branch coverage, but their numerous parameters make it difficult to understand the parameters' impact, leading the user to rely on suboptimal default configurations. While automated tuners have shown promising results, they provide limited insights into why certain configurations work well, motivating the need for Human-in-the-Loop approaches. In this work, we present a visual analytics system, Symetra, designed to support Human-in-the-Loop parameter tuning of symbolic execution engines. To handle a large number of parameters and their configurations, we provide two complementary overviews of their impact on branch coverage values and patterns. Building on these overviews, our system enables collective analysis, allowing the user to contrast groups of configurations and identify differences that may affect branch coverage. We also report on case studies and a Human-in-the-Loop tuning process, demonstrating that experts not only interpreted parameter impacts and identified complementary configurations, but also improved upon fully automated approaches in both branch coverage and tuning efficiency.

</details>


### [25] [OGA-AID: Clinician-in-the-loop AI Report Drafting Assistant for Multimodal Observational Gait Analysis in Post-Stroke Rehabilitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05360)
*Khoi T. N. Nguyen,Nghia D. Nguyen,Hui Yu Koh,Patrick W. H. Kwong,Karen Sui Geok Chua,Ananda Sidarta,Baosheng Yu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: OGA-AID是一个临床医生参与的多智能体大语言模型系统，用于从步态视频和运动捕捉数据自动生成结构化康复报告，显著减少临床医生的工作负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑卒中后步态分析在康复中至关重要，但目前方法耗时且认知负担重，临床医生需要整合步态视频、运动捕捉数据和临床资料来撰写结构化报告。

Method: 开发了OGA-AID系统，采用临床医生参与的多智能体大语言模型架构，协调3个专业智能体来合成患者运动记录、运动学轨迹和临床资料，生成结构化评估报告。

Result: 在真实患者数据上由专业物理治疗师评估，OGA-AID始终优于单次多模态基线方法，错误率低。在临床医生参与模式下，简短的专家初步笔记进一步降低了与参考评估的误差。

Conclusion: 研究证明了多模态智能体系统在结构化临床步态评估中的可行性，并强调了AI辅助分析与人类临床判断在康复工作流程中的互补关系。

Abstract: Gait analysis is essential in post-stroke rehabilitation but remains time-intensive and cognitively demanding, especially when clinicians must integrate gait videos and motion-capture data into structured reports. We present OGA-AID, a clinician-in-the-loop multi-agent large language model system for multimodal report drafting. The system coordinates 3 specialized agents to synthesize patient movement recordings, kinematic trajectories, and clinical profiles into structured assessments. Evaluated with expert physiotherapists on real patient data, OGA-AID consistently outperforms single-pass multimodal baselines with low error. In clinician-in-the-loop settings, brief expert preliminary notes further reduce error compared to reference assessments. Our findings demonstrate the feasibility of multimodal agentic systems for structured clinical gait assessment and highlight the complementary relationship between AI-assisted analysis and human clinical judgment in rehabilitation workflows.

</details>


### [26] [AI and Collective Decisions: Strengthening Legitimacy and Losers' Consent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05368)
*Suyash Fulay,Prerna Ravi,Emily Kubin,Shrestha Mohanty,Michiel Bakker,Deb Roy*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: AI系统通过半结构化访谈收集个人经历，可视化展示政策支持预测与经历，提升集体决策的程序合法性，即使参与者未获偏好结果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在集体决策中主要关注规模与效率，但较少关注程序合法性，特别是"失败者同意"问题——即使未获偏好结果，参与者是否仍认为决策公平。研究旨在探索AI如何基于参与者的不同经历和信念来支撑集体决策，以及接触这些经历是否能增加信任、理解和社会凝聚力

Method: 构建了一个系统：1) 使用半结构化AI访谈员收集政策话题上的个人经历；2) 交互式可视化展示预测的政策支持度与这些经历。通过随机实验(n=181)评估系统效果，所有参与者都遇到与自身偏好相反的决策结果

Result: 与可视化交互增加了感知合法性、对结果的信任以及对他人的理解，尽管所有参与者都遇到了与自身偏好相反的决策结果。这表明AI不仅能提升民主过程的规模和效率，还能增加参与者间的信任和联系

Conclusion: 该工具的设计和评估为未来研究提供了方向，表明AI可以帮助不仅实现民主过程的规模和效率，还能增加参与者间的信任和联系，特别关注程序合法性和"失败者同意"问题

Abstract: AI is increasingly used to scale collective decision-making, but far less attention has been paid to how such systems can support procedural legitimacy, particularly the conditions shaping losers' consent: whether participants who do not get their preferred outcome still accept it as fair. We ask: (1) how can AI help ground collective decisions in participants' different experiences and beliefs, and (2) whether exposure to these experiences can increase trust, understanding, and social cohesion even when people disagree with the outcome. We built a system that uses a semi-structured AI interviewer to elicit personal experiences on policy topics and an interactive visualization that displays predicted policy support alongside those voiced experiences. In a randomized experiment (n = 181), interacting with the visualization increased perceived legitimacy, trust in outcomes, and understanding of others' perspectives, even though all participants encountered decisions that went against their stated preferences. Our hope is that the design and evaluation of this tool spurs future researchers to focus on how AI can help not only achieve scale and efficiency in democratic processes, but also increase trust and connection between participants.

</details>


### [27] [SpeakSoftly: Scaffolding Nonviolent Communication in Intimate Relationships through LLM-Powered Just-In-Time Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05382)
*Ka I Chan,Hongbo Lan,Jun Fang,Yuntao Wang,Yuanchun Shi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: SpeakSoftly是一个基于LLM的系统，应用非暴力沟通原则，通过即时干预帮助伴侣改善冲突沟通，包含NVC-Prompt和NVC-Guide两个核心功能，提供三种干预模式，研究发现不同模式在不同场景下效果不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本沟通中冲突频发，尤其在亲密关系中，误解容易升级为言语攻击。需要开发系统来帮助伴侣在冲突沟通中应用非暴力沟通原则，防止冲突升级。

Method: 基于伴侣访谈和非暴力沟通原则，设计两个核心功能：NVC-Prompt（检测言语攻击并建议修改）和NVC-Guide（分析对话揭示感受和需求）。实现三种渐进干预模式：基本提醒、中性指导、共情指导。采用混合方法用户研究，18对伴侣在模拟和真实冲突场景中评估各模式效果。

Result: 共情指导模式显著促进行为和认知改变；中性指导模式仅在模拟冲突中对行为改变有效；真实冲突中，中性指导因认知负荷较低而表现出独特优势。

Conclusion: 不同干预模式在不同冲突场景下各有优势，共情指导综合效果最佳，但真实冲突中需考虑认知负荷因素。研究为高风险沟通情境中的即时干预设计提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Conflicts are common in text-based communication, particularly in intimate relationships, where misunderstandings can easily escalate into verbal aggression. To address this, we present SpeakSoftly, a system that applies Nonviolent Communication (NVC) principles to scaffold couples' conflict communication through LLM-powered just-in-time interventions. Informed by formative interviews with couples and NVC principles, we designed two core features: NVC-Prompt, which detects verbal aggression and suggests revisions to prevent escalation, and NVC-Guide, which analyzes dialogues to uncover users' feelings and needs, fostering self-awareness and perspective-taking. These features were implemented across three progressive intervention modes, each varying in intervention depth and tone: Basic Reminder, Neutral Guide, and Empathetic Guide. We conducted a mixed-methods user study with 18 couples across simulated and real-life conflict settings to evaluate the effectiveness of each mode. Results showed that Empathetic Guide significantly facilitated both behavioral and cognitive changes, while Neutral Guide was effective only for behavioral changes in simulated conflicts. In real-life conflicts, Neutral Guide showed distinct advantages due to lower cognitive load demands. We discuss the mechanisms behind these findings and propose design implications for in-situ interventions in high-stakes communication contexts.

</details>


### [28] [Transient Non-Use: How People in Migration Experience Digital Disconnection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05386)
*Jonathan Leuenberger,Anamika Rajendran,Augusto Penzo Jara,Tajwar-Ul Hoque,Shiva Darian*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究移民群体在迁移过程中对信息通信技术(ICT)的有意和无意识不使用现象，提出非使用作为保护策略和系统性排斥的应对方式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HCI研究通常关注移民群体的技术采用，但较少关注技术非使用实践。移民经历跨国界、技术和社会系统的多重过渡，需要理解他们如何有意或无意地避免、保留或不使用ICT

Method: 对美国德克萨斯州埃尔帕索边境城市的32名移民进行访谈(2025年2月至5月)，识别设备、信息和保护性非使用等非使用体验，并将非使用概念置于理解、协商和解决三个过渡阶段中分析

Result: 识别了多种非使用体验，展示了ICT非使用如何随时间、风险和制度需求而变化。非使用既作为保护策略，也作为系统性排斥的应对方式

Conclusion: 提出设计原则，将非使用视为有意和无意识的设计条件而非惩罚性失败，强调在设计过程中预期非使用的重要性

Abstract: People experiencing migration endure many transitions across borders, technologies, and social systems. While HCI research often emphasizes this community's adoption of technology, less attention has been paid to practices of technological non-use. This paper investigates how information and communication technologies (ICTs) are intentionally and unintentionally avoided, withheld, or not used during migration. Drawing on interviews with 32 people experiencing migration in the border city of El Paso, Texas, USA between February and May 2025, we identify a range of non-use experiences, including device, informational, and protective non-use. We extend the concept of non-use by situating it within the three phases of transitions: understanding, negotiating, and resolving. We show how ICT non-use shifts with time, risk, and institutional demands. Our analysis demonstrates that non-use functions both as a protective strategy and as a response to systemic exclusion, and concludes with design principles that anticipate non-use as both intentional and unintentional design conditions rather than as punitive failure.

</details>


### [29] [Foreign Domestic Workers' Perspectives on an LLM-Based Emotional Support tool for Caregiving Burden](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05448)
*Shin Shoon Nicholas Teng,Kenny Tsu Wei Choo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 探索性定性研究：在新加坡的外籍家庭佣工如何与LLM驱动的聊天机器人互动，作为日常非临床情感支持工具


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外籍家庭佣工在居家养老中扮演核心角色，但面临语言障碍、社会孤立和支持资源有限等挑战，导致情感照护负担沉重。虽然家庭照护者的负担已有广泛研究，但对外籍家庭佣工如何使用情感支持技术知之甚少。

Method: 采用探索性定性研究方法，通过访谈和引导式聊天机器人互动，对新加坡外籍家庭佣工与LLM驱动聊天机器人的互动体验进行归纳式主题分析。

Result: 识别出三个与设计相关的主题：1) 聊天机器人被体验为心理安全且情感验证的工具；2) 通过适应不完善和碎片化的语言支持语言可及性；3) 被挪用为多功能资源，提供安慰、指导和陪伴。

Conclusion: 研究为设计LLM驱动的情感支持工具提供了重要启示，强调应关注心理安全、可及性和灵活挪用性，以更好地支持外籍家庭佣工的情感需求。

Abstract: Foreign Domestic Workers (FDWs) play a central role in home-based eldercare yet often experience substantial emotional caregiving burden shaped by linguistic barriers, social isolation, and limited access to support. While caregiving burden has been extensively studied among familial caregivers, little is known about how FDWs engage with emotional support technologies. We present an exploratory qualitative study of how FDWs in Singapore interact with a Large Language Model (LLM)-driven chatbot as an everyday, non-clinical form of emotional support. Through interviews and guided chatbot interactions, we conducted an inductive thematic analysis of participants' experiences. We identify three design-relevant themes: chatbots were experienced as psychologically safe and emotionally validating; they supported linguistic accessibility by accommodating imperfect and fragmented language; and they were appropriated as multifunctional resources for reassurance, guidance, and companionship. We discuss implications for designing LLM-driven emotional support tools that foreground psychological safety, accessibility, and flexible appropriation.

</details>


### [30] [How Much Trust is Enough? Towards Calibrating Trust in Technology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05658)
*Gabriela Beltrão,Debora F. de Souza,Sonia Sousa,David Lamas*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过实证研究为Human-Computer Trust Scale (HCTS)开发了实用的解释指南，强调在HCI中需要校准对技术的信任评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着技术日益普及、自主且不透明，用户难以理解系统的能力和限制，需要重新定义人机交互中的信任角色。现有信任评估工具缺乏实用的解释指南。

Method: 采用实证研究方法，基于Human-Computer Trust Scale (HCTS)开发解释指南，详细说明决策过程和理论基础，强调信任评估需要结合具体交互情境。

Result: 研究发现HCTS是评估信任倾向的有前景工具，但评估结果需要反思和解释，必须在具体交互情境中考虑。研究提供了实用的证据基础解释指南。

Conclusion: HCTS可作为初步评估信任倾向的工具，但信任评估需要情境化解释。研究为人机交互中的技术信任校准提供了实践指导，强调信任评估的动态性和情境依赖性。

Abstract: The role of trust within Human-Computer Interaction is being redefined. With the increasing omnipresence, autonomy, and opacity of technology, users often struggle to understand the capabilities and limitations of systems. In this article, we present the results of an empirical study designed to provide a practical, evidence-based interpretation of trust propensity assessment using the Human-Computer Trust Scale (HCTS). We outline the process used to develop a guideline for interpreting the instrument's results and explain the rationale for our decisions, advocating for calibrating trust in technology within HCI. Our findings demonstrate that the HCTS is a promising tool for conducting an initial evaluation of propensity to trust, but that such an assessment requires reflection and interpretation that should be considered within the context of the interaction.

</details>


### [31] [Improving Explanations: Applying the Feature Understandability Scale for Cost-Sensitive Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05790)
*Nicola Rossberg,Bennett Kleinberg,Barry O'Sullivan,Luca Longo,Andrea Visentin*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种在可解释人工智能中同时优化模型准确性和特征可理解性的方法，通过特征可理解性量表和协同优化策略，在保持高分类性能的同时生成更易于理解的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能的普及，机器学习模型的可解释性变得越来越重要。现有的基于自然语言的解释方法通常依赖输入特征来证明推理结果，但用户理解这些解释的能力取决于他们对输入特征的理解程度。因此需要量化特征层面的可理解性，并探索如何在保持模型准确性的同时提高解释的可理解性。

Method: 本研究基于Rossberg等人提出的特征可理解性量表，在两个数据集上收集可理解性评分，提出了一种可理解性和准确性的协同优化方法，并展示了优化后的解释结果和模型准确性。

Result: 研究发现准确性和可理解性可以成功协同优化，同时保持高分类性能。优化后的解释在表面价值上被认为更易于理解。研究为模型可解释性设计提供了知识贡献。

Conclusion: 该概念验证研究表明，通过特征可理解性量表和协同优化方法，可以在不牺牲模型准确性的情况下提高解释的可理解性。未来的研究将通过用户评估来进一步验证这些发现，推动可解释人工智能的发展。

Abstract: With the growing pervasiveness of artificial intelligence, the ability to explain the inferences made by machine learning models has become increasingly important. Numerous techniques for model explainability have been proposed, with natural-language textual explanations among the most widely used approaches. When applied to tabular data, these explanations typically draw on input features to justify a given inference. Consequently, a user's ability to interpret the explanation depends on their understanding of the input features. To quantify this feature-level understanding, Rossberg et al. introduced the Feature Understandability Scale. Building on that work, this proof-of-concept study collects understandability scores across two datasets, proposes a co-optimisation methodology of understandability and accuracy and presents the resulting explanations alongside the model accuracies. This work contributes to the body of knowledge on model interpretability by design. It is found that accuracy and understandability can be successfully co-optimised while maintaining high classification performances. The resulting explanations are considered more understandable at face value. Further research will aim to confirm these findings through user evaluation.

</details>


### [32] [FEEL: Quantifying Heterogeneity in Physiological Signals for Generalizable Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05926)
*Pragya Singh,Ankush Gupta,Somay Jalan,Mohan Kumar,Pushpendra Singh*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: FEEL是首个大规模EDA和PPG信号情感识别基准研究，评估了19个公开数据集上的16种架构，发现微调对比信号-语言预训练模型表现最佳，手工特征优于原始信号，并分析了跨数据集泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生理信号情感识别在心理健康和情感感知系统中有重要应用潜力，但缺乏跨异构数据集的大规模标准化评估限制了进展和模型泛化能力。

Method: 引入FEEL基准研究，使用19个公开数据集评估16种架构，涵盖传统机器学习、深度学习和自监督预训练方法，分为四个建模范式。进行数据集内和跨数据集评估，分析实验设置、设备类型和标注策略变化下的泛化能力。

Result: 微调对比信号-语言预训练模型在唤醒度和效价分类任务中F1分数最高（71/114），而随机森林、LDA和MLP等简单模型仍具竞争力（36/114）。手工特征模型（107/114）持续优于原始信号模型。跨数据集分析显示：真实环境数据训练的模型能很好泛化到实验室（F1=0.79）和约束环境（F1=0.78）；专家标注数据能有效迁移到刺激标注（F1=0.72）和自报告数据集（F1=0.76）；实验室设备训练的模型对定制可穿戴设备（F1=0.81）和Empatica E4（F1=0.73）具有高可迁移性。

Conclusion: 该研究为生理信号情感识别建立了首个大规模基准，证明了微调预训练模型的优越性，强调了领域知识在低资源嘈杂环境中的价值，并揭示了不同实验条件、设备类型和标注策略下的模型泛化模式，为未来研究提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Emotion recognition from physiological signals has substantial potential for applications in mental health and emotion-aware systems. However, the lack of standardized, large-scale evaluations across heterogeneous datasets limits progress and model generalization. We introduce FEEL, the first large-scale benchmarking study of emotion recognition using electrodermal activity (EDA) and photoplethysmography (PPG) signals across 19 publicly available datasets. We evaluate 16 architectures spanning traditional machine learning, deep learning, and self-supervised pretraining approaches, structured into four representative modeling paradigms. Our study includes both within-dataset and cross-dataset evaluations, analyzing generalization across variations in experimental settings, device types, and labeling strategies. Our results showed that fine-tuned contrastive signal-language pretraining (CLSP) models (71/114) achieve the highest F1 across arousal and valence classification tasks, while simpler models like Random Forests, LDA, and MLP remain competitive (36/114). Models leveraging handcrafted features (107/114) consistently outperform those trained on raw signal segments, underscoring the value of domain knowledge in low-resource, noisy settings. Further cross-dataset analyses reveal that models trained on real-life setting data generalize well to lab (F1 = 0.79) and constraint-based settings (F1 = 0.78). Similarly, models trained on expert-annotated data transfer effectively to stimulus-labeled (F1 = 0.72) and self-reported datasets (F1 = 0.76). Moreover, models trained on lab-based devices also demonstrated high transferability to both custom wearable devices (F1 = 0.81) and the Empatica E4 (F1 = 0.73), underscoring the influence of heterogeneity. More information about FEEL can be found on our website https://alchemy18.github.io/FEEL_Benchmark/.

</details>


### [33] [Regimes of Scale in AI Meteorology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06000)
*Anya Martin,Cindy Lin*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 分析AI/ML工具在气象学领域的应用挑战，揭示"尺度机制"冲突导致AI/ML与气象学数据管道整合困难


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HCI研究关注AI/ML在各应用领域的整合，但缺乏对气象学这一已有大数据和物理模型领域的专门分析。研究旨在探索AI/ML在气象预报中的实际应用挑战和冲突

Method: 通过对12位与AI/ML天气建模有不同关联的气象预报员和气象学家进行访谈，分析AI/ML在气象应用中的紧张关系，提出"尺度机制"概念框架

Result: 发现AI/ML与气象学在观测、数据和模型尺度方面存在根本性冲突，AI/ML方法源于特定平台和互联网基础设施，难以与气象学不同的数据管道组织方式整合

Conclusion: AI/ML不应被视为领域无关的工具，其方法源于特定基础设施背景，在与气象学等不同领域整合时需要重新思考数据管道组织方式，而非简单应用

Abstract: HCI work has explored the effective integration of AI/ML tools across "application domains" from healthcare to finance to transportation. We add to this literature with an analysis of AI/ML tools in meteorology, a domain that already uses "big data" and massive physics-based models. Drawing from 12 interviews with forecasters and meteorologists with varied connections to AI/ML weather modeling, we trace tensions in AI/ML weather application arising from what we call "regimes of scale," different ways that AI/ML and meteorological regimes make observations, data, and models scale. Rather than seeing AI/ML as a domain-agnostic tool, we argue that AI/ML methods were born from specific platform and internet infrastructures, and so they can struggle to integrate with very different (in this case meteorological) ways of organizing data pipelines.

</details>


### [34] [Designing Around Stigma: Human-Centered LLMs for Menstrual Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06008)
*Amna Shahnawaz,Ayesha Shafique,Ding Wang,Maryam Mustafa*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 开发基于WhatsApp的LLM+RAG聊天机器人，为巴基斯坦女性提供月经健康教育，通过共同设计解决文化禁忌和资源不足问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巴基斯坦的月经健康教育受到文化禁忌和正式课程不足的限制，女性缺乏可信赖的资源。需要开发文化敏感的解决方案来应对这些挑战

Method: 1) 与30名巴基斯坦女大学生进行研讨会，确定设计需求；2) 开发基于WhatsApp的LLM+RAG聊天机器人，支持罗马乌尔都语，使用补贴平台，包含专家策划的知识库；3) 部署给13名参与者使用两周，收集403条消息并进行访谈

Result: 女性使用聊天机器人挑战文化禁忌，将常被忽视的健康问题合法化，通过迭代提问建立生殖健康知识。但也暴露了问题：依赖文化解释模型、信任与验证问题、聊天机器人本身的性别化人格

Conclusion: 贡献包括：实证洞察、针对文化敏感对话AI的污名感知设计框架、以及在私密健康领域强调专家验证的方法论视角

Abstract: Menstrual health education (MHE) in Pakistan is constrained by cultural taboos and inadequate formal curricula, leaving women with few trusted resources to lean on. In response to these challenges, we introduce a WhatsApp-based chatbot powered by a large language model (LLM) and Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG), co-designed with Pakistani college women. Workshops (N=30) revealed key design requirements -- support for Roman Urdu, use of subsidized platforms, and an expert -- curated knowledge base. We then deployed the chatbot with 13 participants for two weeks (403 messages and interviews). Women used it to challenge cultural taboos, legitimize health concerns often dismissed as normal, and build reproductive health knowledge through iterative questioning. Yet, interactions also exposed tensions: reliance on cultural explanatory models, questions of trust and validation, and gendered persona of the chatbot itself. We contribute empirical insights, a stigma-aware design framework for culturally sensitive conversational AI, and a methodological lens foregrounding expert validation in intimate health domains.

</details>


### [35] [Understanding Educators' Perceptions of AI-generated Non-consensual Intimate Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06131)
*Tongxin Li,Katelyn M Reyes,Liezeil Jimenez,Katie S Nam,Donghee Yvette Wohn*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究通过访谈20名美国教育工作者，探讨学校应对AI生成非自愿亲密图像(AIG-NCII)的需求、挑战和支持措施


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AIG-NCII是AI技术进步带来的新兴社会问题，近期中小学事件凸显其紧迫性，但学校应对此类问题的具体支持需求尚不明确

Method: 采用访谈研究方法，对美国20名教育工作者进行访谈，调查他们对AIG-NCII的态度、经验和实践

Result: 教育工作者担忧学生和自身的脆弱性，学校现有实践有限，缺乏培训和系统政策，面临资源不足、法律边界不清、AI知识有限等挑战

Conclusion: 研究结果为交互式教育工具设计、课程设计和政策制定提供参考，强调需要多方利益相关者策略来应对AIG-NCII相关问题

Abstract: AI-generated non-consensual intimate imagery (AIG-NCII) is an emerging social problem due to the advancement of AI tools. While recent incidents in middle and high schools have highlighted the urgency of this issue, there is limited understanding of what concrete supports schools need to effectively address AIG-NCII. To fill this gap, we conducted an interview study with 20 educators in the U.S. and investigated their attitudes, experiences, and practices related to AIG-NCII. Educators expressed concerns about both students' and their own vulnerability, as AIG-NCII may cause moral decline among students, while educators themselves could become victims. Nevertheless, existing practices in schools are limited, and they lack both training and systematic policies. Challenges such as a lack of resources, unclear legal boundaries, and limited knowledge of AI make implementation difficult. The findings of this paper contribute to interactive educational tool design, curriculum design, and policy-making, especially regarding the need for multi-stakeholder strategies to address issues surrounding AIG-NCII.

</details>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [36] [From Video to Control: A Survey of Learning Manipulation Interfaces from Temporal Visual Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04974)
*Linfang Zheng,Zikai Ouyang,Chen Wang,Jia Pan,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文综述了利用无动作标注的时序视频学习机器人操作控制接口的方法，提出了基于接口的三种分类方法，并分析了机器人集成层的关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频作为物理动力学的可扩展观测，捕捉了物体运动、接触展开和场景演化等时序结构，但缺乏动作监督且与机器人经验存在差异。如何将这种时序结构转化为可靠的机器人控制是一个开放挑战。

Method: 提出了接口中心的分类法，将视频到控制接口的方法分为三类：1) 直接视频-动作策略（接口隐式）；2) 潜在动作方法（通过紧凑学习中间层路由时序结构）；3) 显式视觉接口（预测可解释目标供下游控制使用）。分析了每种方法的控制集成特性。

Result: 通过跨家族综合分析发现，最紧迫的开放挑战集中在机器人集成层——将视频衍生预测连接到可靠机器人行为的机制。识别了三种方法家族在闭环方式、执行前验证能力和故障引入位置等方面的差异。

Conclusion: 视频到机器人控制的关键瓶颈在于机器人集成层，需要研究如何将视频预测可靠地连接到实际机器人行为。提出了填补这一空白的研究方向，强调需要开发更稳健的集成机制来确保视频衍生控制在实际机器人系统中的可靠性。

Abstract: Video is a scalable observation of physical dynamics: it captures how objects move, how contact unfolds, and how scenes evolve under interaction -- all without requiring robot action labels. Yet translating this temporal structure into reliable robotic control remains an open challenge, because video lacks action supervision and differs from robot experience in embodiment, viewpoint, and physical constraints. This survey reviews methods that exploit non-action-annotated temporal video to learn control interfaces for robotic manipulation. We introduce an \emph{interface-centric taxonomy} organized by where the video-to-control interface is constructed and what control properties it enables, identifying three families: direct video--action policies, which keep the interface implicit; latent-action methods, which route temporal structure through a compact learned intermediate; and explicit visual interfaces, which predict interpretable targets for downstream control. For each family, we analyze control-integration properties -- how the loop is closed, what can be verified before execution, and where failures enter. A cross-family synthesis reveals that the most pressing open challenges center on the \emph{robotics integration layer} -- the mechanisms that connect video-derived predictions to dependable robot behavior -- and we outline research directions toward closing this gap.

</details>


### [37] [COMB: Common Open Modular robotic platform for Bees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04980)
*Pranav Kedia,Marie Messerich,Tim Landgraf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: COMB是一个紧凑、开源、模块化的机电平台，专为在标准观察蜂箱框架内进行蜂巢内实验而设计，集成了XY定位平台、可移动访问窗口、可互换负载模块和嵌入式控制架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要能够在有限空间约束下操作、耐受蜂巢特定污染和环境条件、支持传感和局部驱动而无需频繁硬件重新设计的机器人系统，以实现对真实蜂群的实验访问。

Method: 开发了COMB平台，包括：1）XY定位平台；2）可移动访问窗口（MAW），用于通过蜂巢边界进行密封工具访问；3）可互换负载模块；4）嵌入式控制架构，支持可重复轨迹执行和信号生成。通过三个代表性模块展示能力：仿生舞蹈和信号负载、近距离蜂巢扫描仪、用于局部振荡刺激的电磁翼驱动器。

Result: 平台在工程术语上进行了表征：1）跟踪摇摆轨迹执行；2）执行多图像拼接以生成重复蜂巢马赛克；3）对翼驱动器进行基于视频的光谱分析。这些结果表明COMB可作为可重复使用的实验机器人平台，用于受控的蜂巢内传感和驱动。

Conclusion: COMB是一个紧凑、通用的后继平台，取代了早期特定任务的蜜蜂机器人系统，为在真实蜂巢环境中进行受控实验提供了可重复使用的机器人平台。

Abstract: Experimental access to real honeybee colonies requires robotic systems capable of operating within limited spatial constraints, tolerating hive-specific fouling and environmental conditions, and supporting both sensing and localized actuation without frequent hardware redesign. This paper introduces COMB, a compact, open-source, modular mechatronic platform designed for in-hive experiments within standard observation-hive frames. The platform integrates a XY positioning stage, a Movable Access Window (MAW) for sealed tool access through the hive boundary, interchangeable payload modules, and an embedded control architecture that enables repeatable trajectory execution and signal generation. The platform's capabilities are demonstrated through three representative modules: a biomimetic dance-and-signaling payload, a close-range comb scanner, and an electromagnetic wing actuator for localized oscillatory stimulation. This paper details the hardware and software design of COMB, outlines its operational capabilities, and describes the supporting infrastructure for conducting real-world in-hive experiments. The platform is characterized in engineering terms through tracking waggle-trajectory executions, performing multi-image stitching for repeated comb mosaics, and conducting video-based spectral analysis of the wing actuator. These results position COMB as a reusable experimental robotics platform for controlled in-hive sensing and actuation, and as a compact, generalized successor to earlier task-specific honeybee robotic systems.

</details>


### [38] [A Survey on Sensor-based Planning and Control for Unmanned Underwater Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05003)
*Shivam Vishwakarma,Tejal Bedmutha,Dharmendra Kumar Patel,Vijay Bhaskar Semwal,Leena Vachhani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述系统回顾了无人水下航行器（UUV）基于传感器的规划与控制方法，重点关注复杂水下环境中的实时自适应导航、动态避障和重规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UUV在复杂、不确定的水下环境中面临多重挑战：传感器测量噪声大、缺乏GNSS信号、水下声学通信带宽低且延迟高。这些限制要求开发先进的规划与控制策略，以实现有效的自主导航和动态环境适应能力。

Method: 综述将现有文献分为解耦架构和耦合架构两类。解耦架构顺序处理规划与控制阶段，而耦合架构提供更紧密的反馈回路以实现更快的响应。重点分析基于实时传感器输入（如声纳和IMU）的局部反应式规划层，以提高定位精度和自主性。

Result: 比较分析显示：PID控制器简单但缺乏复杂机动的预测能力；MPC提供优越的路径优化但计算量大；不变集控制器提供强安全保证但可能牺牲狭窄环境中的敏捷性。综述提出了结合规划与控制的架构分类法，并分析了控制器在集成规划框架中的作用。

Conclusion: 该综述为UUV基于传感器的规划与控制方法提供了系统分类和分析框架，强调了自适应局部规划和紧密集成控制回路对于提高UUV在动态水下环境中的自主导航能力的重要性，为未来研究提供了理论基础和方向指导。

Abstract: This survey examines recent sensor-based planning and control methods for Unmanned Underwater Vehicles (UUVs). In complex, uncertain underwater environments, UUVs require advanced planning and control strategies for effective navigation. These vehicles face significant challenges including drifting and noisy sensor measurements, absence of Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) signals, and low-bandwidth, high-latency underwater acoustic communications. The focus is on reactive local planning layers that adapt to real-time sensor inputs such as SONAR and Inertial Measurement Units (IMU) to improve localization accuracy and autonomy in dynamic ocean conditions, enabling dynamic obstacle avoidance and on-the-fly re-planning. The survey categorizes the existing literature into decoupled and coupled architectures for sensor-based planning and control. The decoupled architecture sequentially addresses planning and control stages, whereas coupled architectures offer tighter feedback loops for more immediate responsiveness. A comparative analysis of coupled planning and control methods reveals that while PID controllers are simple, they lack predictive capability for complex maneuvers. Model Predictive Control (MPC) offers superior path optimization but can be computationally intensive, and invariant-set controllers provide strong safety guarantees at the potential cost of agility in confined environments. Key contributions include a taxonomy of architectures combining planning and control, a focus on adaptive local planning, and an analysis of controller roles in integrated planning frameworks for autonomous navigation of UUVs.

</details>


### [39] [StarVLA: A Lego-like Codebase for Vision-Language-Action Model Developing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05014)
*StarVLA Community*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StarVLA是一个开源的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）研究代码库，提供模块化架构、可复用训练策略和统一评估接口，旨在解决现有VLA方法在架构、代码库和评估协议上的碎片化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建通用具身智能体需要整合感知、语言理解和动作能力，但现有的VLA方法存在架构不兼容、代码库分散和评估协议不统一的问题，阻碍了方法的比较和复现。

Method: StarVLA采用三方面设计：1）模块化的骨干-动作头架构，支持VLM骨干（如Qwen-VL）和世界模型骨干（如Cosmos）以及多种动作解码范式；2）可复用的训练策略，包括跨具身学习和多模态协同训练；3）统一评估接口，集成多个主流基准测试（LIBERO、SimplerEnv等），支持仿真和真实机器人部署。

Result: StarVLA提供了简单、完全可复现的单基准训练方案，在最小数据工程的情况下，已在多个基准测试上达到或超越了现有方法，支持VLM和世界模型骨干。

Conclusion: StarVLA是目前最全面的开源VLA框架之一，有望降低现有方法复现和新方法原型开发的障碍，项目将持续维护和扩展。

Abstract: Building generalist embodied agents requires integrating perception, language understanding, and action, which are core capabilities addressed by Vision-Language-Action (VLA) approaches based on multimodal foundation models, including recent advances in vision-language models and world models. Despite rapid progress, VLA methods remain fragmented across incompatible architectures, codebases, and evaluation protocols, hindering principled comparison and reproducibility. We present StarVLA, an open-source codebase for VLA research. StarVLA addresses these challenges in three aspects. First, it provides a modular backbone--action-head architecture that supports both VLM backbones (e.g., Qwen-VL) and world-model backbones (e.g., Cosmos) alongside representative action-decoding paradigms, all under a shared abstraction in which backbone and action head can each be swapped independently. Second, it provides reusable training strategies, including cross-embodiment learning and multimodal co-training, that apply consistently across supported paradigms. Third, it integrates major benchmarks, including LIBERO, SimplerEnv, RoboTwin~2.0, RoboCasa-GR1, and BEHAVIOR-1K, through a unified evaluation interface that supports both simulation and real-robot deployment. StarVLA also ships simple, fully reproducible single-benchmark training recipes that, despite minimal data engineering, already match or surpass prior methods on multiple benchmarks with both VLM and world-model backbones. To our best knowledge, StarVLA is one of the most comprehensive open-source VLA frameworks available, and we expect it to lower the barrier for reproducing existing methods and prototyping new ones. StarVLA is being actively maintained and expanded; we will update this report as the project evolves. The code and documentation are available at https://github.com/starVLA/starVLA.

</details>


### [40] [GaussFly: Contrastive Reinforcement Learning for Visuomotor Policies in 3D Gaussian Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05062)
*Yuhang Zhang,Mingsheng Li,Yujing Shang,Zhuoyuan Yu,Chao Yan,Jiaping Xiao,Mir Feroskhan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GaussFly是一个用于自主飞行器视觉运动策略学习的新框架，通过3D高斯溅射重建训练场景，结合对比表示学习提取鲁棒特征，实现高效的模拟到真实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于单目视觉的端到端学习方法存在样本效率低和模拟到真实差距大的问题，主要原因是高维RGB观测直接映射到动作命令，以及模拟与真实域之间的视觉差异。

Method: 采用"真实-模拟-真实"范式，将表示学习与策略优化解耦：1) 使用3D高斯溅射结合显式几何约束重建训练场景，实现高保真实到模拟转换；2) 在逼真模拟环境中应用对比表示学习，从渲染的RGB图像中提取紧凑、抗噪声的潜在特征；3) 使用预训练编码器提供低维特征输入，降低视觉运动策略计算负担并增强抗视觉噪声能力。

Result: 在模拟和真实环境中的广泛实验表明，GaussFly相比基线方法具有更优的样本效率和渐近性能。关键的是，它能够实现零样本策略迁移到未见过的真实世界环境中，有效弥合模拟到真实差距。
