Both benchmarks concentrate over 90\% of evaluation on Python while TypeScript, GitHub's most-used language, is absent. Backend and frontend development, which together constitute 46% of real-world editing activity, are largely missing, and documentation, testing, and maintenance edits (31.4% of human PRs) have zero representation. Both benchmarks have modest test counts (CanItEdit median 13, EDIT-Bench median 4), though CanItEdit compensates with near-complete whole-file coverage and fail-before/pass-after validation. 59\% of EDIT-Bench's low-coverage suites would not detect modifications outside the edit region. EDIT-Bench has 15 problems that are not solved by any of 40 LLMs and 11 of these problems trace failures to poor benchmark artifacts rather than model limitations. Further, 29% of EDIT-Bench problems and 6% of CanItEdit problems share a codebase with at least one other problem within the benchmark. In summary, these benchmarks measure a narrower construct than deployment decisions require. We therefore propose six empirically grounded desiderata and release all audit artifacts so the community can build instructed code-editing benchmarks whose scores reliably reflect real-world editing capability.

</details>


### [78] [A Multi-Agent Framework for Automated Exploit Generation with Constraint-Guided Comprehension and Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05130)
*Siyi Chen,Tianhan Luo,Shijian Wu,Xiangyu Liu,Yilin Zhou,Qi Li,Wenyuan Xu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Vulnsage是一个基于多智能体框架的自动化漏洞利用生成系统，通过模拟安全研究人员的工作流程，将复杂的AEG过程分解为多个专门子智能体，显著提高了漏洞利用生成的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源库在现代软件开发中广泛使用，引入了大量安全漏洞。静态分析工具虽然能大规模识别潜在漏洞，但通常产生大量误报。传统的自动化漏洞利用生成方法（基于模糊测试或符号执行）面临路径覆盖和约束求解问题。尽管LLM在AEG方面显示出巨大潜力，但如何有效利用它们理解漏洞并生成相应利用仍是一个开放问题。

Method: Vulnsage采用多智能体框架，模拟人类安全研究人员的工作流程，将复杂AEG过程分解为多个专门子智能体：代码分析智能体、代码生成智能体、验证智能体和一组反思智能体，由中央监督器通过迭代循环协调。代码分析智能体执行静态分析识别潜在漏洞并收集相关信息；代码生成智能体利用LLM生成候选利用；验证智能体和反思智能体形成反馈驱动的自我优化循环，使用执行轨迹和运行时错误分析来迭代改进利用或推理误报警报。

Result: 实验评估表明，Vulnsage比最先进的工具（如explodejs）多生成34.64%的利用。此外，Vulnsage在真实场景中成功发现并验证了146个零日漏洞，证明了其在软件供应链安全评估中的实际有效性。

Conclusion: Vulnsage通过多智能体框架有效解决了传统AEG方法的局限性，显著提高了漏洞利用生成的成功率，并在真实场景中验证了其实际应用价值，为软件供应链安全评估提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Open-source libraries are widely used in modern software development, introducing significant security vulnerabilities. While static analysis tools can identify potential vulnerabilities at scale, they often generate overwhelming reports with high false positive rates. Automated Exploit Generation (AEG) emerges as a promising solution to confirm vulnerability authenticity by generating an exploit. However, traditional AEG approaches based on fuzzing or symbolic execution face path coverage and constraint-solving problems. Although LLMs show great potential for AEG, how to effectively leverage them to comprehend vulnerabilities and generate corresponding exploits is still an open question.
  To address these challenges, we propose Vulnsage, a multi-agent framework for AEG. Vulnsage simulates human security researchers' workflows by decomposing the complex AEG process into multiple specialized sub-agents: Code Analyzer Agent, Code Generation Agent, Validation Agent, and a set of Reflection Agents, orchestrated by a central supervisor through iterative cycles. Given a target program, the Code Analyzer Agent performs static analysis to identify potential vulnerabilities and collects relevant information for each one. The Code Generation Agent then utilizes an LLM to generate candidate exploits. The Validation Agent and Reflection Agents form a feedback-driven self-refinement loop that uses execution traces and runtime error analysis to either improve the exploit iteratively or reason about the false positive alert.
  Experimental evaluation demonstrates that Vulnsage succeeds in generating 34.64\% more exploits than state-of-the-art tools such as \explodejs. Furthermore, Vulnsage has successfully discovered and verified 146 zero-day vulnerabilities in real-world scenarios, demonstrating its practical effectiveness for assisting security assessment in software supply chains.

</details>


### [79] [Compiled AI: Deterministic Code Generation for LLM-Based Workflow Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05150)
*Geert Trooskens,Aaron Karlsberg,Anmol Sharma,Lamara De Brouwer,Max Van Puyvelde,Matthew Young,John Thickstun,Gil Alterovitz,Walter A. De Brouwer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 编译式AI：通过LLM在编译阶段生成可执行代码，实现确定性执行，提高可靠性、可审计性和成本效益，特别适用于医疗等高要求企业工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决高要求企业工作流（特别是医疗领域）中对可靠性、可审计性、成本效益和安全性的需求。传统运行时LLM调用存在不确定性、成本高和安全风险，需要一种更可控的AI部署范式。

Method: 提出编译式AI系统架构：1）受限LLM代码生成架构；2）四阶段生成-验证流水线（将概率模型输出转化为生产就绪代码）；3）评估框架（衡量代币摊销、确定性、可靠性、安全性和成本）。在函数调用（BFCL）和文档智能（DocILE）任务上进行评估。

Result: 函数调用任务：96%任务完成率，零执行代币消耗，约17笔交易时与运行时推理成本持平，1000笔交易时代币消耗减少57倍。文档智能任务：Code Factory变体在关键字段提取（KILE: 80.0%）上与直接LLM相当，同时获得最高行项目识别准确率（LIR: 80.4%）。安全评估：135个测试案例中，提示注入检测准确率96.7%，静态代码安全分析准确率87.5%，零误报。

Conclusion: 编译式AI通过将LLM推理从运行时移至编译时，为高要求企业工作流提供了可靠、可审计、成本效益高且安全的解决方案。该方法在函数调用和文档智能任务上表现出色，特别适用于医疗等对可靠性要求严格的领域。

Abstract: We study compiled AI, a paradigm in which large language models generate executable code artifacts during a compilation phase, after which workflows execute deterministically without further model invocation. This paradigm has antecedents in prior work on declarative pipeline optimization (DSPy) and hybrid neural-symbolic planning (LLM+P); our contribution is a systems-oriented study of its application to high-stakes enterprise workflows, with particular emphasis on healthcare settings where reliability and auditability are critical.
  By constraining generation to narrow business-logic functions embedded in validated templates, compiled AI trades runtime flexibility for predictability, auditability, cost efficiency, and reduced security exposure. We introduce (i) a system architecture for constrained LLM-based code generation, (ii) a four-stage generation-and-validation pipeline that converts probabilistic model output into production-ready code artifacts, and (iii) an evaluation framework measuring operational metrics including token amortization, determinism, reliability, security, and cost.
  We evaluate on two task types: function-calling (BFCL, n=400) and document intelligence (DocILE, n=5,680 invoices). On function-calling, compiled AI achieves 96% task completion with zero execution tokens, breaking even with runtime inference at approximately 17 transactions and reducing token consumption by 57x at 1,000 transactions. On document intelligence, our Code Factory variant matches Direct LLM on key field extraction (KILE: 80.0%) while achieving the highest line item recognition accuracy (LIR: 80.4%). Security evaluation across 135 test cases demonstrates 96.7% accuracy on prompt injection detection and 87.5% on static code safety analysis with zero false positives.

</details>


### [80] [Corporate Training in Brazilian Software Engineering: A Quantitative Study of Professional Perceptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05263)
*Rodrigo Siqueira,Antonio Oliveira,Breno Alves Andrade,Lidiane C. S. Gomes,Danilo Monteiro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过定量调查发现，软件工程企业培训质量与效果主要受认知参与度、活动多样性和讲师表现三个紧密相关因素影响，强制参与会显著降低培训质量感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管企业战略培训对软件工程师持续专业发展至关重要，但目前缺乏从专业人员视角评估培训质量与效果影响因素的研究工具，在软件工程领域尤其缺乏经过验证的评估工具。

Method: 采用定量调查方法，对282名巴西软件工程专业人员进行了结构化问卷调查，使用多分相关分析进行数据处理。

Result: 研究发现三个紧密相关的因素（认知参与度、活动多样性和讲师表现）是培训质量与效果感知的最强预测因子；强制参与显著降低动机和培训质量感知；个人时间影响感知与培训质量基本独立。

Conclusion: 软件工程培训效果主要由认知参与度、活动多样性和讲师表现决定；强制参与对动机、相关性和培训质量感知产生负面影响；研究结果与一般培训文献一致，表明软件组织无需重新设计培训原则，可放心应用现有指南；Salas和Cannon-Bowers框架在SE环境中表现一致，适合未来心理测量验证。

Abstract: Context: Strategic corporate training is essential for the sustained professional development of software engineers. However, there is a knowledge gap regarding the factors that drive quality and effectiveness of such training from the professionals' perspective, and no validated instrument exists for assessing these factors in the software engineering (SE) domain. Objective: This study aims to quantitatively analyze which factors influence SE professionals' perceptions of corporate training quality and effectiveness. Method: A quantitative survey was conducted with 282 Brazilian SE professionals. A structured questionnaire was developed and polychoric correlation was adopted for data analysis. Results: Three tightly correlated factors (cognitive engagement, variety of activities, and instructor performance) emerged as the strongest predictors of perceived training quality and effectiveness. Mandatory participation significantly reduces motivation and perceived training quality. Perceived impact on personal time proved to be largely independent of training quality. These findings are consistent with the general training effectiveness literature. Conclusions: Training effectiveness in the SE context is predominantly determined by three factors: cognitive engagement, variety of activities, and instructor performance. Mandatory participation negatively influences motivation, perceived relevance, and perceived training quality, while also amplifying the perception of time burden. The consistency with the general literature suggests that software organizations do not need to reinvent training design principles and can apply established guidelines with confidence. Salas and Cannon-Bowers' framework produced coherent results in the SE context, making it a promising candidate for future psychometric validation.

</details>


### [81] [FLARE: Agentic Coverage-Guided Fuzzing for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05289)
*Mingxuan Hui,Xinyue Li,Lu Wang,Chengcheng Wan,Yifan Wang,Yimian Wang,Feiyue Song,Beining Shi,Yixi Li,Yaxiao Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FLARE是一个针对多智能体LLM系统的测试框架，通过提取智能体规范、构建测试预言器、进行覆盖引导的模糊测试来检测系统故障，显著提高了测试覆盖率并发现了大量未知故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统在自动化复杂工作流程时经常出现故障（如无限循环、工具调用失败），但由于缺乏智能体规范、行为空间庞大以及基于语义的正确性判断，传统软件测试技术难以有效检测这些故障。

Method: FLARE以MAS源代码为输入，从智能体定义中提取规范和构建行为空间；基于这些规范构建测试预言器，进行覆盖引导的模糊测试以暴露故障；分析执行日志判断测试是否通过，并生成故障报告。

Result: 在16个多样化开源应用上的评估显示：FLARE实现了96.9%的智能体间覆盖率和91.1%的智能体内覆盖率，分别比基线高出9.5%和1.0%；发现了56个MAS特有的先前未知故障。

Conclusion: FLARE是一个有效的多智能体LLM系统测试框架，能够显著提高测试覆盖率并检测传统方法难以发现的故障，为MAS的可靠性提供了重要保障。

Abstract: Multi-Agent LLM Systems (MAS) have been adopted to automate complex human workflows by breaking down tasks into subtasks. However, due to the non-deterministic behavior of LLM agents and the intricate interactions between agents, MAS applications frequently encounter failures, including infinite loops and failed tool invocations. Traditional software testing techniques are ineffective in detecting such failures due to the lack of LLM agent specification, the large behavioral space of MAS, and semantic-based correctness judgment.
  This paper presents FLARE, a novel testing framework tailored for MAS. FLARE takes the source code of MAS as input and extracts specifications and behavioral spaces from agent definitions. Based on these specifications, FLARE builds test oracles and conducts coverage-guided fuzzing to expose failures. It then analyzes execution logs to judge whether each test has passed and generates failure reports. Our evaluation on 16 diverse open-source applications demonstrates that FLARE achieves 96.9% inter-agent coverage and 91.1% intra-agent coverage, outperforming baselines by 9.5% and 1.0%. FLARE also uncovers 56 previously unknown failures unique to MAS.

</details>


### [82] [On the Role of Fault Localization Context for LLM-Based Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05481)
*Melika Sepidband,Hung Viet Pham,Hadi Hemmati*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过大规模实证分析发现，在基于LLM的自动程序修复中，增加故障定位上下文并不总是提升修复性能。文件级定位是主要影响因素，提供15-17倍的性能提升，而元素级和行级上下文扩展的效果有限甚至可能降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 故障定位是基于LLM的自动程序修复的关键组件，但其影响尚未得到充分探索。研究者希望明确：需要多少定位信息、超出预测错误位置的额外上下文是否有益、以及如何检索这些上下文。

Method: 在500个SWE-bench验证实例上使用GPT-5-mini进行大规模实证研究，评估了61种配置，这些配置在文件级、元素级和行级上下文方面存在差异。

Result: 更多上下文并不一致地改善修复性能。文件级定位是主导因素，相比无文件基线提升15-17倍。扩展文件上下文通常与性能改善相关，成功修复最常见于包含约6-10个相关文件的配置。元素级上下文扩展提供条件性增益，强烈依赖于文件上下文质量；行级上下文扩展常因噪声放大而降低性能。基于LLM的检索通常优于结构启发式方法，同时使用更少的文件和标记。

Conclusion: 最有效的故障定位上下文策略通常结合高层次抽象的广泛语义理解和精确的行级定位。这些发现挑战了"增加定位上下文能统一改善自动程序修复"的假设，并为设计基于LLM的故障定位策略提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Fault Localization (FL) is a key component of Large Language Model (LLM)-based Automated Program Repair (APR), yet its impact remains underexplored. In particular, it is unclear how much localization is needed, whether additional context beyond the predicted buggy location is beneficial, and how such context should be retrieved. We conduct a large-scale empirical study on 500 SWE-bench Verified instances using GPT-5-mini, evaluating 61 configurations that vary file-level, element-level, and line-level context. Our results show that more context does not consistently improve repair performance. File-level localization is the dominant factor, yielding a 15-17x improvement over a no-file baseline. Expanding file context is often associated with improved performance, with successful repairs most commonly observed in configurations with approximately 6-10 relevant files. Element-level context expansion provides conditional gains that depend strongly on the file context quality, while line-level context expansion frequently degrades performance due to noise amplification. LLM-based retrieval generally outperforms structural heuristics while using fewer files and tokens. Overall, the most effective FL context strategy typically combines a broad semantic understanding at higher abstraction levels with precise line-level localization. These findings challenge our assumption that increasing the localization context uniformly improves APR, and provide practical guidance for designing LLM-based FL strategies.

</details>


### [83] [SCOPE: A Dataset of Stereotyped Prompts for Counterfactual Fairness Assessment of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05555)
*Alessandra Parziale,Gianmario Voria,Valeria Pontillo,Andrea De Lucia,Gemma Catolino,Fabio Palomba*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SCOPE：大规模反事实提示对数据集，用于系统评估LLMs中的群体敏感行为，涵盖120,640个提示对、1,438个主题、9个偏见维度、1,536个人口群体和4种交流意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLMs公平性评估资源主要依赖小型模板化反事实示例或固定句子对，存在语言多样性有限、主题覆盖狭窄、缺乏对交流意图如何影响模型行为的分析支持等问题。

Method: 构建SCOPE数据集，包含241,280个提示，组织成120,640个反事实对，基于1,438个主题，涵盖9个偏见维度、1,536个人口群体，并在4种不同交流意图（问题、推荐、指示、澄清）下生成。

Result: 创建了一个大规模、受控、语义对齐且意图感知的数据集，为评估LLMs的公平性、鲁棒性和反事实一致性提供了系统化基础。

Conclusion: SCOPE解决了现有公平性评估基准的局限性，提供了一个全面、多样化且意图感知的资源，能够更系统地调查LLMs中的群体敏感行为。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) now serve as the foundation for a wide range of applications, from conversational assistants to decision support tools, making the issue of fairness in their results increasingly important. Previous studies have shown that LLM outputs can shift when prompts reference different demographic groups, even when intent and semantic content remain constant. However, existing resources for probing such disparities rely primarily on small, template-based counterfactual examples or fixed sentence pairs. These benchmarks offer limited linguistic diversity, narrow topical coverage, and little support for analyzing how communicative intent affects model behavior. To address these limitations, we introduce SCOPE (Stereotype-COnditioned Prompts for Evaluation), a large-scale dataset of counterfactual prompt pairs designed to enable systematic investigation of group-sensitive behavior in LLMs. SCOPE contains 241,280 prompts organized into 120,640 counterfactual pairs, each grounded in one of 1,438 topics and spanning nine bias dimensions and 1,536 demographic groups. All prompts are generated under four distinct communicative intents: Question, Recommendation, Direction, and Clarification, ensuring broad coverage of common interaction styles. This resource provides a controlled, semantically aligned, and intent-aware basis for evaluating fairness, robustness, and counterfactual consistency.

</details>


### [84] [An Iterative Test-and-Repair Framework for Competitive Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05560)
*Lingxiao Tang,Muyang Ye,Zhaoyang Chu,Xiaoxue Ren,Zhongxin Liu,Lingfeng Bao,He Ye*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FixAudit是一个用于竞争性编程代码生成的新框架，通过迭代测试-修复调试循环改进代码质量，解决了现有方法中测试不针对具体实现、代码无法基于失败测试修复的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的代码生成方法（如CURE）存在两个关键限制：1) 测试器不读取候选代码，无法暴露实现特定的错误；2) 编码器从头生成每个候选代码，无法基于失败测试修复有错误的程序。需要一种能够迭代改进代码质量的新方法。

Method: FixAudit采用迭代测试-修复调试循环：从单个初始候选代码开始，通过四个阶段训练一个共享模型，包含两个专门角色：修复器（基于失败测试修复当前候选代码）和审计器（读取候选代码生成暴露其剩余错误的新测试）。

Result: 在APPS、CodeContests和xCodeEval三个基准测试上，基于7B模型的FixAudit在零样本设置下超越了同模型家族32B基线的平均性能。与相同7B基础模型的强基线相比，FixAudit将平均Pass@1提高了35.1%到36.8%，平均AvgPassRatio提高了7.1%到24.5%。

Conclusion: FixAudit通过引入迭代测试-修复调试循环，显著改进了竞争性编程中的代码生成质量，证明了针对具体实现进行测试和基于失败测试修复代码的有效性，为代码生成提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have made remarkable progress in code generation, but competitive programming remains a challenge. Recent training-based methods have improved code generation by using reinforcement learning (RL) with execution feedback. The more recent framework CURE further incorporates test generation into the training process, jointly training a Coder and a Tester within a single model. At inference time, the Coder generates many candidate programs, and the Tester generates tests from the problem description. The candidate who passes the most of the generated tests is selected as the final answer. However, CURE has two critical limitations. First, the Tester never reads any candidate code, so its tests often fail to expose implementation-specific bugs. Second, the Coder generates every candidate from scratch and never learns to fix a buggy program based on a failed test. To address these limitations, we propose FixAudit, which approaches competitive code generation from a new perspective: starting from a single initial candidate, it iteratively improves the candidate through a targeted test-and-repair debugging cycle. The framework trains one shared model with two specialized roles through four stages: the Fixer, which repairs the current candidate based on a failing test, and the Auditor, which reads the candidate code to generate new tests that expose its remaining bugs. We evaluate FixAudit on three benchmarks: APPS, CodeContests, and xCodeEval. Applied to a 7B model, the framework surpasses the average performance of the larger 32B baseline within the same model family under the zero-shot setting. Compared to strong baselines built on the same 7B base model, FixAudit improves average Pass@1 by 35.1% to 36.8% and average AvgPassRatio by 7.1% to 24.5%.

</details>


### [85] [Bias Ahead: Sensitive Prompts as Early Warnings for Fairness in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05575)
*Gianmario Voria,Martina De Lucia,Alessandra Raia,Andrea De Lucia,Gemma Catolino,Fabio Palomba*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出"敏感提示"作为LLM公平性评估的新抽象概念，构建了包含12,801个提示的SensY数据集，发现LLM在回答敏感提示时经常忽略伦理和情境因素，并开发了自动分类器来预测提示敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平性基准主要关注刻板印象特定关联，限制了在多样化现实场景中预测风险的能力。需要一种新方法来评估LLM在敏感内容上的表现，实现从被动缓解向预防性设计的转变。

Method: 提出"敏感提示"作为公平性评估的新抽象概念；构建SensY数据集（12,801个提示，分为敏感和非敏感类别，覆盖7个主题领域，结合合成生成和真实用户输入）；使用该数据集查询3个开源LLM，手动分析4,500个响应；开发自动分类器预测提示敏感性。

Result: 模型通常能提供事实正确的答案，但经常未能承认敏感输入的伦理、关系或情境含义；自动分类器在敏感提示上表现出稳健性能；提示敏感性可作为LLM公平性风险的有效早期预警机制。

Conclusion: 提示敏感性视角将公平性评估从被动缓解转向预防性设计，使开发者能够在部署前预测和管理偏见，为LLM公平性评估提供了新的方法论框架。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are being increasingly integrated into software systems, offering powerful capabilities but also raising concerns about fairness. Existing fairness benchmarks, however, focus on stereotype-specific associations, which limit their ability to anticipate risks in diverse, real-world contexts. In this paper, we propose sensitive prompts as a new abstraction for fairness evaluation: inputs that are not inherently biased but are more likely to elicit biased or inadequate responses due to the sensitivity of their content. We construct and release SensY, a dataset of 12,801 prompts, categorized as sensitive and non-sensitive, spanning seven thematic domains, combining synthetic generation and real user inputs. Using this dataset, we query three open-source LLMs and manually analyze 4,500 responses to evaluate their adequacy in answering sensitive prompts. Results show that while models often provide factually correct answers, they frequently fail to acknowledge the ethical, relational, or contextual implications of sensitive inputs. In addition, we develop an automated classifier for predicting prompt sensitivity, achieving robust performance on sensitive prompts. Our findings demonstrate that prompt sensitivity can serve as an effective early-warning mechanism for fairness risks in LLMs. This perspective shifts fairness assessment from reactive mitigation toward preventive design, enabling developers to anticipate and manage bias before deployment.

</details>


### [86] [Understanding: reframing automation and assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05662)
*Robin Bloomfield*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出将"理解"作为系统安全与保证决策的明确、可评估和可辩护的组成部分，以应对当前技术发展带来的理解挑战，并基于Elgin的理解认识论和Assurance 2.0框架提出了具体工程实现方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前安全与保证案例存在与负责任工程和治理决策所需理解脱节的风险。技术发展带来的压力（节奏加快、审查减少、软件复杂性增加、AI生成产物增多）可能导致产出看似连贯但缺乏真正人类理解支持，形成"理解挑战"。

Method: 基于Catherine Elgin的理解认识论建立概念基础，利用Assurance 2.0作为工程实现路径，通过结构化论证、证据、置信度、反驳因素和基于理论的自动化来操作化。创建两个关联产物：理解基础（证明可用理解对决策的充分性）和个人理解声明（参与者明确表达其理解并接受挑战）。

Result: 提出了将理解作为决策明确组成部分的框架，包括概念基础、工程实现方法和具体产物。同时识别了自动化可能改善产物生产但削弱理解的风险，并提出了评估有效性和认识论影响的初步方向。

Conclusion: 理解应成为关键社会技术系统决策中明确、可评估和可辩护的组成部分。通过提出的概念框架和工程方法，可以应对当前技术发展带来的理解挑战，确保决策基于真正的理解而非表面的连贯性。

Abstract: Safety and assurance cases risk becoming detached from the understanding needed for responsible engineering and governance decisions. More broadly, the production and evaluation of critical socio-technical systems increasingly face an understanding challenge: pressures for increased tempo, reduced scrutiny, software complexity, and growing use of AI generated artefacts may produce outputs that appear coherent without supporting genuine human comprehension. We argue that understanding should become an explicit, assessable, and defensible component of decision making: what developers, assessors, and decision makers grasp about system behavior, evidence, assumptions, risks, and residual uncertainty. Drawing on Catherine Elgin's epistemology of understanding, we outline a conceptual foundation and then use Assurance 2.0 as an engineering route to operationalize using structured argumentation, evidence, confidence, defeaters, and theory based automation. This leads to two linked artefacts: an Understanding Basis, which justifies why available understanding is sufficient for a decision, and a Personal Understanding Statement, through which participants make their grasp explicit and challengeable. We also identify risks that automation may improve artefact production while weakening understanding, and we propose initial directions for evaluating both efficacy and epistemic impact.

</details>


### [87] [CAKE: Cloud Architecture Knowledge Evaluation of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05755)
*Tim Lukas Adam,Phongsakon Mark Konrad,Riccardo Terrenzi,Florian Girardo Lukas,Rahime Yilmaz,Krzysztof Sierszecki,Serkan Ayvaz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CAKE是一个评估LLM对云原生软件架构理解的基准测试，包含188个专家验证问题，涵盖Bloom分类法的四个认知层次和五个云原生主题。评估22个模型配置后发现：MCQ准确率在3B参数以上趋于稳定，最佳达99.2%；自由回答分数在所有认知层次上持续提升；两种格式捕获不同知识维度；推理增强提升自由回答质量，工具增强对小模型有负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏评估大型语言模型对云原生软件架构实际理解能力的基准测试。LLM作为软件架构副驾驶的角色日益重要，但现有基准无法有效衡量其在架构设计、分析、实现等认知层次上的能力。

Method: 开发CAKE基准测试，包含188个专家验证问题，覆盖Bloom修订分类法的四个认知层次（回忆、分析、设计、实现）和五个云原生主题。评估22个模型配置（0.5B-70B参数），涵盖四个LLM家族。对多项选择题采用三轮多数投票，对自由回答采用LLM-as-a-judge评分。同时测试推理增强（+think）和工具增强（+tool）的效果。

Result: 1) MCQ准确率在3B参数以上趋于稳定，最佳模型达99.2%；2) 自由回答分数在所有认知层次上持续提升；3) 两种评估格式捕获不同知识维度，MCQ接近天花板而自由回答仍能区分模型能力；4) 推理增强提升自由回答质量，工具增强对小模型有负面影响。

Conclusion: 评估格式从根本上影响对LLM架构知识的测量方式。MCQ适合基础知识评估但存在天花板效应，自由回答能更好区分高阶认知能力。推理增强有效提升模型表现，而工具增强需要谨慎使用，特别是对小模型。CAKE基准为评估LLM的云原生架构理解提供了全面框架。

Abstract: In today's software architecture, large language models (LLMs) serve as software architecture co-pilots. However, no benchmark currently exists to evaluate large language models' actual understanding of cloud-native software architecture. For this reason we present a benchmark called CAKE, which consists of 188 expert-validated questions covering four cognitive levels of Bloom's revised taxonomy -- recall, analyze, design, and implement -- and five cloud-native topics. Evaluation is conducted on 22 model configurations (0.5B--70B parameters) across four LLM families, using three-run majority voting for multiple-choice questions (MCQs) and LLM-as-a-judge scoring for free-responses (FR). Based on this evaluation, four notable findings were identified. First, MCQ accuracy plateaus above 3B parameters, with the best model reaching 99.2\%. Second, free-response scores scale steadily across all cognitive levels. Third, the two formats capture different facets of knowledge, as the MCQ accuracy approaches a ceiling while free-responses continue to differentiate models. Finally, reasoning augmentation (+think) improves free-response quality, while tool augmentation (+tool) degrades performance for small models. These results suggest that the evaluation format fundamentally shapes how we measure architectural knowledge in LLMs.

</details>


### [88] [An Empirical Study of Perceptions of General LLMs and Multimodal LLMs on Hugging Face](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05782)
*Yujian Liu,Xiao Yu,Jacky Keung,Xing Hu,Xin Xia,Xiaoxue Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析Hugging Face平台上的用户讨论，系统性地识别了大型语言模型（LLMs）和多模态LLMs（MLLMs）在实际使用中的主要用户关切点，并提出了改进LLM生态系统的可行建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着通用LLMs（GLLMs）和多模态LLMs（MLLMs）的广泛应用，理解真实场景下的用户感知变得日益重要。现有研究多依赖问卷调查或特定平台数据（如Reddit或GitHub issues），这些方法要么通过预设问题限制用户反馈，要么过度强调故障驱动的调试讨论，无法捕捉实践中多样化、经验驱动和跨模型的用户视角。

Method: 研究在Hugging Face这一主要模型中心进行实证分析，收集了来自38个代表性模型（21个GLLMs和17个MLLMs）的662个讨论线程，并进行手动标注。开发了一个三级分类法来系统性地描述用户关切点。

Result: 分析显示，LLM访问障碍、生成质量、部署和调用复杂性是最突出的关切点，同时还包括文档限制和资源约束等问题。研究还发现了GLLMs和MLLMs用户关注点的差异。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，该研究提出了改进LLM生态系统的可行建议，强调了理解真实用户反馈对于LLM技术发展和应用的重要性，为模型开发者和平台提供了有价值的指导。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have rapidly evolved from general-purpose systems to multimodal models capable of processing text, images, and audio. As both general-purpose LLMs (GLLMs) and multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) gain widespread adoption, understanding user perceptions in real-world settings becomes increasingly important. However, existing studies often rely on surveys or platform-specific data (e.g., Reddit or GitHub issues), which either constrain user feedback through predefined questions or overemphasize failure-driven, debugging-oriented discussions, thus failing to capture diverse, experience-driven, and cross-model user perspectives in practice. To address this issue, we conduct an empirical study of user discussions on Hugging Face, a major model hub with diverse models and active communities. We collect and manually annotate 662 discussion threads from 38 representative models (21 GLLMs and 17 MLLMs), and develop a three-level taxonomy to systematically characterize user concerns. Our analysis reveals that LLM access barriers, generation quality, and deployment and invocation complexity are the most prominent concerns, alongside issues such as documentation limitations and resource constraints. Based on these findings, we derive actionable implications for improving LLM ecosystem.

</details>


### [89] [Reinforcement Learning with Negative Tests as Completeness Signal for Formal Specification Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05820)
*Zhechong Huang,Zhao Zhang,Zeyu Sun,Huifeng Sun,Yingfei Xiong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SpecRL：基于强化学习的规范合成框架，通过生成负测试作为规范完整性的细粒度信号，在Dafny中自动生成程序规范和验证注解


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规范合成任务对程序验证至关重要，但现有验证器反馈不完整——验证通过只能证明正确性，无法区分弱规范和强规范，缺乏规范完整性的细粒度信号

Method: 提出SpecRL强化学习框架，包含自主生成负测试（程序永远无法产生的输入-输出对）的流程，将候选规范拒绝负测试的比例作为规范完整性的信号，并整合到RL训练的奖励中

Result: 在四种模型规模上的实验表明，SpecRL在规范强度和验证成功率上优于SFT和基于二元规范强度奖励的RL，能泛化到分布外基准，在未见基准上与更大的通用LLM保持竞争力

Conclusion: SpecRL通过负测试提供规范完整性的细粒度信号，有效解决了规范合成中验证器反馈不完整的问题，为自动生成强规范和验证注解提供了新方法

Abstract: The specification synthesis task aims to automatically generate specifications, together with any necessary auxiliary verification annotations, for existing programs. This task is important because such specifications serve as behavioral contracts that support modular reasoning and reusable verification across a codebase. At the same time, it remains challenging because verifier-only feedback is fundamentally incomplete: passing verification establishes soundness, but cannot distinguish weak specifications from strong ones. What is missing is a fine-grained signal for specification completeness.
  We present SpecRL, a reinforcement learning framework for specification synthesis in Dafny. SpecRL introduces a self-contained pipeline that generates negative tests, i.e., input-output pairs that can never be produced by the program. We use the fraction of these negative tests rejected by a candidate specification as a signal of specification completeness, which is integrated into the reward for RL training. Experiments across four model sizes show that SpecRL improves both specification strength and verification success over SFT and RL with a binary specification-strength reward, generalizes to an out-of-distribution benchmark, and remains competitive on that unseen benchmark compared to much larger general-purpose LLMs.

</details>


### [90] [Proof of Concept as a First-Class Architectural Decision Instrument](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05835)
*Bruno Fernando Antognolli,Fabio Petrillo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文针对软件工程中广泛使用但概念模糊的"概念验证"实践，通过系统文献综述提出了精确定义和结构化框架，将其提升为架构决策的一等工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念验证在软件工程中广泛应用，但在研究和工业实践中缺乏清晰一致的定义和方法论，通常被视为非正式实验或一次性制品，影响了决策质量和可追溯性。

Method: 通过系统综述学术文献和灰色文献，识别PoC的关键特征和过程，发现学术文献主要描述结果而忽略过程；基于此提出精确定义和轻量级的三阶段框架（规划、执行、决策），强调技术验证和显式决策可追溯性。

Result: 提出了PoC的精确定义和包含规划、执行、决策三个阶段的框架；识别了"未记录的架构实验"反模式，即PoC影响高影响架构决策但未留下持久的架构知识；论证了将PoC提升为一等工具可改善决策质量、增强可追溯性并支持更系统的架构实践学习。

Conclusion: 将概念验证提升为架构决策的一等工具而非非正式实验，通过结构化框架和显式决策可追溯性，能够显著改善软件架构实践的决策质量和系统性学习。

Abstract: Proofs of Concept (PoCs) are widely adopted practices in software engineering. Despite their relevance, PoCs remain conceptually underdefined and methodologically ad hoc in both research and industry, with definitions and implementation approaches that often lack clarity and consistency. This paper investigates the concept of PoCs with two complementary goals: (1) to provide a refined definition and astructured framework for PoC development grounded in a systematic review of academic and grey literature; and (2) to position PoCs as first-class architectural decision instruments rather than informal experiments or disposable artifacts. Through a systematic review of academic and grey literature we identify the key characteristics, processes, associated with PoCs and expose a significant gap the academic literature describes PoC outcomes but rarely its process. By synthesizing insights from diverse sources we propose a refined definition and a lightweight, three-phase framework (planning, execution, decision-making) that encompasses technical validation and explicit decision traceability. We also introduce the Undocumented Architectural Experiment anti-pattern, arising when PoCs influence high-impact architectural decisions without leaving durable architectural knowledge. We argue that elevating PoCs to first-class status improves decision quality, enhances traceability, and supports more systematic learning in architectural practice.

</details>


### [91] [Evolutionary Optimization of AI-Collapsed Software Development Stacks: Labor Tipping Points and Workforce Realignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05948)
*Matthew H. Kilbane*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一个量化框架来优化软件开发中人类与AI的劳动力分配，该框架可扩展到其他劳动类别。通过建立基准和AI压缩劳动力模型，推导安全裁员临界点方程，并嵌入多目标进化优化设置中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI技术在软件开发领域的应用日益广泛，需要系统性的方法来优化人类与AI的劳动力分配，在降低成本的同时保持工作质量和稳定的工作负荷。

Method: 1. 形式化基准劳动力模型和AI压缩劳动力模型；2. 推导安全裁员临界点方程；3. 将模型嵌入多目标进化优化框架（NSGA-II算法）；4. 进行实验分析阶段特定的自动化策略。

Result: NSGA-II实验揭示了可重复的、阶段特定的自动化策略，这些策略能够降低成本，同时保持工作质量和稳定的工作负荷。

Conclusion: 该量化框架为软件开发中人类与AI劳动力优化提供了系统方法，可识别安全裁员临界点并生成平衡成本、质量和负荷的自动化策略，具有扩展到其他劳动类别的潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents a quantitative framework for optimizing human AI workforce allocation in software development, translatable to other labor categories. I formalize baseline and AI-collapsed labor models, derive tipping point equations for safe headcount reduction, and embed them in a multi objective evolutionary optimization setup. NSGAII experiments reveal reproducible, phase specific automation strategies that reduce cost while maintaining quality and stable workloads.

</details>


### [92] [QiMeng-PRepair: Precise Code Repair via Edit-Aware Reward Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05963)
*Changxin Ke,Rui Zhang,Jiaming Guo,Yuanbo Wen,Li Ding,Shuo Wang,Xuyuan Zhu,Xiong Peng,Di Huang,Zidong Du,Xing Hu,Qi Guo,Yunji Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出PRepair框架解决LLM程序修复中的过度编辑问题，通过精确修复任务最大化重用正确代码，结合自破坏和自修复组件，使用编辑感知奖励优化修复精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在程序修复中表现良好，但存在过度编辑问题，即过度修改会覆盖正确代码并阻碍错误定位。需要系统量化其影响并引入精确修复任务，最大化重用正确代码同时仅修复错误部分。

Method: 提出PRepair框架：1) Self-Breaking组件通过受控错误注入和最小-最大采样生成多样化错误程序；2) Self-Repairing组件使用编辑感知组相对策略优化(EA-GRPO)训练模型，采用编辑感知奖励鼓励最小但正确的编辑。

Result: PRepair在联合考虑修复正确性和程度的fix₁@1指标下，将修复精度提升高达31.4%。结合推测编辑时显著提高解码吞吐量，展示了精确且实用的代码修复潜力。

Conclusion: PRepair框架有效缓解了LLM程序修复中的过度编辑问题，通过精确修复任务和编辑感知优化策略，显著提高了修复精度和效率，为精确实用的代码修复提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve strong program repair performance but often suffer from over-editing, where excessive modifications overwrite correct code and hinder bug localization. We systematically quantify its impact and introduce precise repair task, which maximizes reuse of correct code while fixing only buggy parts. Building on this insight, we propose PRepair, a framework that mitigates over-editing and improves repair accuracy. PRepair has two components: Self-Breaking, which generates diverse buggy programs via controlled bug injection and min-max sampling, and Self-Repairing, which trains models with Edit-Aware Group Relative Policy Optimization (EA-GRPO) using an edit-aware reward to encourage minimal yet correct edits. Experiments show that PRepair improves repair precision by up to 31.4% under $\mathrm{fix}_1@1$, a metric that jointly considers repair correctness and extent, and significantly increases decoding throughput when combined with speculative editing, demonstrating its potential for precise and practical code repair.

</details>

### [93] [Edge Intelligence for Satellite-based Earth Observation: Scheduling Image Acquisition and Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05937)
*Beatriz Soret,Antonio M. Mercado-Martínez,Antonio Jurado-Navas,Nicolai D. Lyholm,Marco Moretti,Petar Popovski,Israel Leyva-Mayorga*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种面向低地球轨道卫星星座的能量感知框架，通过优化观测调度和处理调度，实现遥感数据的实时语义处理，特别针对海上船只检测任务验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代地球观测任务产生海量图像数据，挑战了现有的下行链路和地面处理能力，特别是在时间敏感应用中。需要一种能够利用卫星星座边缘计算资源实现实时语义处理的解决方案。

Method: 提出能量感知框架，包含两个耦合的优化问题：(1)观测调度：选择图像采集机会，考虑湍流引起的图像质量退化和能量预算；(2)处理调度：在星载和地面处理器间分配语义工作负载。框架任务无关，适用于广泛的语义和目标导向推理问题。

Result: 实验表明：任务和湍流感知的观测调度显著提高了观测目标的质量和数量；星座内的协同边缘处理相比传统下行链路架构大幅降低了功耗；量化了YOLOv8在不同计算平台上的执行时间分布。

Conclusion: 分布式边缘智能有潜力增强未来卫星地球观测系统的响应性和自主性，通过优化观测和处理调度实现实时语义处理，特别适用于海上监视等时间敏感应用。

Abstract: Modern Earth Observation (EO) missions generate massive volumes of imagery that challenge existing downlink and ground-processing capabilities, particularly for time-critical applications. This work investigates how a low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite constellation equipped with heterogeneous edge computing resources can enable real-time semantic processing of data acquired by EO satellites. We introduce an energy-aware framework that optimizes the use of resources accounting for data acquisition, computing, and communication constraints. Although we focus on maritime surveillance, the formulation is task-agnostic and accommodates a broad class of semantic and goal-oriented inference problems. Specifically, we formulate two coupled optimization problems: (i) observation scheduling, which selects image acquisition opportunities while accounting for turbulence-induced image degradation and energy budget, and (ii) processing scheduling, which allocates semantic workloads across onboard and ground processors. We evaluate these mechanisms for the task of detection and localization of vessels, for which we quantify the benefits of turbulence-aware observation scheduling for preserving image quality and experimentally characterize the execution-time distribution of YOLOv8 on different computing platforms. Results demonstrate that task- and turbulence-aware observation scheduling can significantly improve the quality and quantity of observed targets. Furthermore, cooperative edge processing within the constellation substantially reduces power consumption compared to traditional downlink-centric architectures. These findings highlight the potential of distributed edge intelligence to enhance the responsiveness and autonomy of future satellite-based EO systems.

</details>


### [94] [Design and Analysis of Chirp-Layered Superposition Coding for LoRa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06033)
*Jingxiang Huang,Samer Lahoud*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出一种用于LoRa的啁啾分层叠加编码方案，通过在标准LoRa传输上线性叠加额外波形来提高频谱效率，同时最小化对标准LoRa解调的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRa作为低功耗广域网技术，其频谱效率有限。本文旨在提高LoRa链路的频谱效率，同时保持相对简单的收发器架构。

Method: 首先分析任何非零叠加信号对标准去啁啾-DFT解调器输出的扰动，然后表征在给定功率预算下最小化这种退化的叠加波形类别。特别地，设计将高扩频因子LoRa波形叠加在低扩频因子信号上（如SF12叠加在SF7上），使对标准LoRa解调的影响保持较小。在每个低SF符号间隔内，高SF段可视为准窄带载波来传输额外的BPSK流。

Result: 推导了低SF LoRa层和叠加高SF层的解析误码率表达式，并通过蒙特卡洛仿真验证。提出的啁啾分层叠加编码方案提高了LoRa链路的频谱效率。

Conclusion: 啁啾分层叠加编码方案能有效提高LoRa链路的频谱效率，同时使用相对简单的收发器架构。高SF波形叠加在低SF信号上的设计使对标准LoRa解调的影响最小化，实现了额外的BPSK数据传输。

Abstract: This paper investigates the design of chirp-layered superposition coding for LoRa, where an additional waveform is linearly superposed on a standard LoRa transmission with minimal impact on the LoRa demodulation process. We first show that any non-zero superposed signal perturbs the output of the standard dechirp-and-DFT demodulator, and then characterize the class of superposed waveforms that minimize this degradation under a given power budget. In particular, we show that a high spreading factor (high-SF) LoRa waveform superposed on a low-SF signal (e.g., SF12 on SF7) can be designed so that its impact on the standard LoRa demodulation remains small. As a result, within each low-SF symbol interval, the high-SF segment can be treated as a quasi-narrowband carrier that conveys an additional BPSK stream. We derive analytical error-rate expressions for both the low-SF LoRa layer and the superposed high-SF layer, and validate them through Monte Carlo simulations. The proposed chirp-layered superposition coding scheme improves the spectral efficiency of LoRa-based links and uses a relatively simple transceiver architecture.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [Guidelines for Producing Concise LNT Models, Illustrated with Formal Models of the Algorand Consensus Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05006)
*Hubert Garavel*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: LNT语言通过引入变量直接赋值、对称顺序组合和显式循环等特性，克服了传统进程演算的局限性，本文展示了如何利用这些特性编写简洁可读的LNT模型，并以Algorand区块链共识协议为例，通过优化将代码行数减少三分之二，同时提升可读性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LNT作为一种现代并发系统形式化描述语言，虽然克服了传统进程演算的局限性，但如何充分利用其特性（如变量直接赋值、对称顺序组合、显式循环等）来编写简洁、可读的模型仍需要系统研究。本文旨在探索如何通过精心设计的转换和优化技术，最大化LNT的表达能力，实现模型简洁性和可读性的双重提升。

Method: 采用案例研究方法，以Algorand区块链共识协议的形式化模型为运行示例，展示如何应用LNT语言特性进行模型优化。通过一系列精心选择的转换技术，包括利用变量直接赋值、对称顺序组合和显式循环等特性，对原始模型进行重构和简化。同时采用多种验证方法：可视化检查、等价性检查和模型检查，来验证优化后模型的正确性和性质。

Result: 通过应用优化转换技术，成功将Algorand共识协议的LNT模型代码行数减少了三分之二（除以3），同时显著提升了模型的可读性。使用可视化检查、等价性检查和模型检查等多种验证方法，成功表达并验证了形式化模型的各种性质，证明了优化后模型在保持语义正确性的同时实现了结构简化。

Conclusion: LNT语言的高级特性（变量直接赋值、对称顺序组合、显式循环）能够有效支持编写简洁且可读的形式化模型。通过精心设计的转换技术，可以显著减少代码量并提升可读性，同时保持模型的语义正确性。多种验证方法的结合使用确保了优化后模型的可靠性，为LNT在实际系统建模中的应用提供了有效指导。

Abstract: LNT is a modern language for the formal description of concurrent systems. It generalizes traditional process calculi and overcomes their known limitations by incorporating features such as an imperative programming style with direct assignments to variables, symmetric sequential composition, and explicit loop operators. The present article examines how these features can be taken advantage of to obtain LNT models as concise and readable as possible. The study is illustrated with a running example, the consensus protocol of the Algorand blockchain, a formal model of which was recently developed at the University of Urbino. It is shown that, using well-chosen transformations, the number of lines of LNT code can be divided by three, while improving readability. Also, various properties of the formal model are expressed and verified using visual checking, equivalence checking, and model checking.

</details>


### [96] [AutoLALA: Automatic Loop Algebraic Locality Analysis for AI and HPC Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05066)
*Yifan Zhu,Yekai Pan,Yanghui Wu,Chen Ding*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: AutoLALA是一个开源工具，用于分析仿射循环程序的数据局部性，通过符号化方法计算重用距离和数据移动复杂度，支持多种高性能计算和AI工作负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现代计算系统中，数据移动是主要性能瓶颈。对于高性能计算和AI工作负载中常见的循环程序（如矩阵乘法、张量收缩、模板计算和einsum操作），数据在内存层次结构中移动的成本通常超过算术运算成本。因此需要工具来分析程序的数据局部性特征。

Method: AutoLALA接受用小型领域特定语言编写的程序，将其降级为多面体集合和映射，通过仿射变换构建时间戳空间和访问映射，使用Barvinok计数操作推导符号化的重用间隔和重用距离分布。系统基于Zhu等人的完全符号局部性分析和Smith等人的数据移动距离框架实现。

Result: 开发了一个用Rust实现的模块化库，包含三个crate，提供命令行界面和交互式Web playground，支持LaTeX渲染输出公式。工具能够处理任意仿射循环嵌套，覆盖张量收缩、einsum表达式、模板计算和一般多面体程序等工作负载。

Conclusion: AutoLALA提供了一个系统化的工具来分析仿射循环程序的数据局部性，通过符号化方法避免了堆栈模拟和Denning的递归工作集公式，为优化数据移动性能提供了理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: Data movement is the primary bottleneck in modern computing systems. For loop-based programs common in high-performance computing (HPC) and AI workloads, including matrix multiplication, tensor contraction, stencil computation, and einsum operations, the cost of moving data through the memory hierarchy often exceeds the cost of arithmetic.
  This paper presents AutoLALA, an open-source tool that analyzes data locality in affine loop programs. The tool accepts programs written in a small domain-specific language (DSL), lowers them to polyhedral sets and maps, and produces closed-form symbolic formulas for reuse distance and data movement complexity. AutoLALA implements the fully symbolic locality analysis of Zhu et al. together with the data movement distance (DMD) framework of Smith et al. In particular, it computes reuse distance as the image of the access space under the access map, avoiding both stack simulation and Denning's recursive working-set formulation.
  We describe the DSL syntax and its formal semantics, the polyhedral lowering pipeline that constructs timestamp spaces and access maps via affine transformations, and the sequence of Barvinok counting operations used to derive symbolic reuse-interval and reuse-distance distributions. The system is implemented in Rust as a modular library spanning three crates, with safe bindings to the Barvinok library.
  We provide both a command-line interface and an interactive web playground with LaTeX rendering of the output formulas. The tool handles arbitrary affine loop nests, covering workloads such as tensor contractions, einsum expressions, stencil computations, and general polyhedral programs.

</details>


### [97] [EffiPair: Improving the Efficiency of LLM-generated Code with Relative Contrastive Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05137)
*Samira Hajizadeh,Suman Jana*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: EffiPair：基于相对对比反馈的推理时迭代优化框架，无需模型微调即可提升LLM生成代码的运行效率和内存效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖单一程序的绝对执行反馈（如运行时分析），成本高且优化指导性弱。LLM生成的代码虽然功能正确，但在运行时间和内存使用上往往效率低下。

Method: 提出相对对比反馈（RCF）机制，通过对比同一任务下结构相似的两个程序，识别与效率提升相关的差异。基于此构建EffiPair框架：生成多个候选方案，识别效率差距大的程序对，将执行差异总结为轻量级反馈，利用该信号生成更高效的解决方案。

Result: 在代码效率基准测试中，EffiPair在保持正确性的同时持续提升效率。使用DeepSeek-Chat V3.2时，相比无性能反馈的生成，EffiPair实现最高1.5倍加速，相比先前工作减少90%以上的token使用量。

Conclusion: 通过用成对对比比较替代孤立的标量反馈，EffiPair提供了更直接的优化指导，同时降低了性能分析和提示开销，为LLM代码效率优化提供了有效的推理时解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often generate code that is functionally correct but inefficient in runtime and memory. Prior approaches to improving code efficiency typically rely on absolute execution feedback, such as profiling a single program's runtime or memory usage, which is costly and provides weak guidance for refinement. We propose Relative Contrastive Feedback (RCF), an inference-time feedback mechanism that requires no model fine-tuning or parameter updates. RCF compares two structurally similar programs for the same task and highlights the differences associated with better efficiency. Building on this idea, we introduce EffiPair, an inference-time iterative refinement framework that operates entirely at test time by generating multiple candidate solutions, identifying informative program pairs with large efficiency gaps, summarizing their execution differences into lightweight feedback, and using this signal to produce more efficient solutions. By replacing isolated scalar feedback with pairwise contrastive comparisons, EffiPair provides more direct guidance while reducing profiling and prompting overhead. Experiments on code-efficiency benchmarks show that EffiPair consistently improves efficiency while preserving correctness. For instance, with DeepSeek-Chat V3.2, EffiPair achieves up to 1.5x speedup over generation without performance feedback, while reducing token usage by more than 90% compared to prior work.

</details>


### [98] [A Gradual Probabilistic Lambda Calculus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05246)
*Wenjia Ye,Matías Toro,Federico Olmedo*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了GPLC，一种渐进类型化的概率λ演算，支持在静态和动态类型检查之间平滑过渡，并首次将渐进类型化应用于概率编程语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率编程语言在机器学习和差分隐私等领域应用广泛，通常依赖类型系统进行静态检查。然而，严格的类型系统可能过于繁琐或保守。渐进类型化支持静态与动态检查的平滑过渡，但从未在概率语言中得到探索。

Method: 设计并形式化GPLC，包含二元概率选择算子，允许程序员逐步引入/移除静态类型和概率标注。静态语义基于概率耦合概念，动态语义通过细化到目标语言TPLC实现，后者采用基于分布的语义将程序解释为最终值的概率分布。

Result: 建立了TPLC（因此也包括GPLC）的类型安全性，并满足渐进语言的精炼标准：是静态变体的保守扩展，满足渐进保证，在类型精度方面表现平滑。

Conclusion: 首次将渐进类型化应用于概率编程语言，提出了GPLC并建立了其理论基础，为概率编程提供了更灵活的类型检查方法。

Abstract: Probabilistic programming languages have recently gained a lot of attention, in particular due to their applications in domains such as machine learning and differential privacy. To establish invariants of interest, many such languages include some form of static checking in the form of type systems. However, adopting such a type discipline can be cumbersome or overly conservative.
  Gradual typing addresses this problem by supporting a smooth transition between static and dynamic checking, and has been successfully applied for languages with different constructs and type abstractions. Nevertheless, its benefits have never been explored in the context of probabilistic languages.
  In this work, we present and formalize GPLC, a gradual source probabilistic lambda calculus. GPLC includes a binary probabilistic choice operator and allows programmers to gradually introduce/remove static type -- and probability -- annotations. The static semantics of GPLC heavily relies on the notion of probabilistic couplings, as required for defining several relations, such as consistency, precision, and consistent transitivity. The dynamic semantics of GPLC is given via elaboration to the target language TPLC, which features a distribution-based semantics interpreting programs as probability distributions over final values. Regarding the language metatheory, we establish that TPLC -- and therefore also GPLC -- is type safe and satisfies two of the so-called refined criteria for gradual languages, namely, that it is a conservative extension of a fully static variant and that it satisfies the gradual guarantee, behaving smoothly with respect to type precision.

</details>


### [99] [Proceedings 17th Workshop on Programming Language Approaches to Concurrency and Communication-cEntric Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05737)
*Kirstin Peters,Lorenzo Gheri*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: PLACES 2026研讨会论文集，聚焦并发和通信中心软件的程序语言方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为应对并发和分布式编程的挑战，改进现代和未来计算机应用的基础，提供一个交流新思想的论坛

Method: 作为ETAPS的卫星活动，汇集来自不同领域的研究人员，涵盖编程语言设计、并发分布式系统模型、类型系统、程序验证等主题

Result: 包含PLACES 2026研讨会的会议论文集，该研讨会将于2026年4月11日在意大利都灵举行

Conclusion: PLACES为并发和通信中心软件的程序语言方法研究提供了重要的学术交流平台，促进该领域的理论发展和实践应用

Abstract: This volume contains the proceedings of PLACES 2026, the 17th edition of the Workshop on Programming Language Approaches to Concurrency and Communication-cEntric Software. The workshop is scheduled to take place in Turin, Italy, on April 11, 2026, as a satellite event of ETAPS, the European Joint Conferences on Theory and Practice of Software. PLACES offers a forum for exchanging new ideas on how to address the challenges of concurrent and distributed programming and how to improve the foundations of modern and future computer applications. PLACES welcomes researchers from various fields, and its topics include the design of new programming languages, models for concurrent and distributed systems, type systems, program verification, and applications in various areas (e.g., microservices, sensor networks, blockchains, event processing, business process management).

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Territory Paint Wars: Diagnosing and Mitigating Failure Modes in Competitive Multi-Agent PPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04983)
*Diyansha Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Territory Paint Wars环境，系统研究了PPO在自博弈中的失败模式，识别了五个实现层面的问题，发现了竞争性过拟合现象，并提出对手混合方法进行缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究竞争性多智能体强化学习中PPO算法的失败模式，特别是在自博弈设置下，为理解MARL中的常见问题提供系统性的分析框架。

Method: 1. 开发Territory Paint Wars环境作为最小竞争性MARL基准；2. 通过控制性消融实验识别五个实现层面的失败模式；3. 发现竞争性过拟合现象；4. 提出对手混合干预方法（20%训练回合使用固定随机策略）。

Result: 1. 初始训练84000回合后仅获得26.8%胜率；2. 修正五个失败模式后自博弈性能提升至73.5%；3. 发现竞争性过拟合导致泛化胜率从73.5%降至21.6%；4. 对手混合方法将泛化性能恢复至77.1%（±12.6%，10次种子）。

Conclusion: 竞争性MARL中存在多种实现层面的失败模式，竞争性过拟合是自博弈中难以检测的严重问题，简单的对手混合方法能有效缓解过拟合并提升泛化性能，Territory Paint Wars为研究MARL失败模式提供了可复现的基准。

Abstract: We present Territory Paint Wars, a minimal competitive multi-agent reinforcement learning environment implemented in Unity, and use it to systematically investigate failure modes of Proximal Policy Optimisation (PPO) under self-play. A first agent trained for $84{,}000$ episodes achieves only $26.8\%$ win rate against a uniformly-random opponent in a symmetric zero-sum game. Through controlled ablations we identify five implementation-level failure modes -- reward-scale imbalance, missing terminal signal, ineffective long-horizon credit assignment, unnormalised observations, and incorrect win detection -- each of which contributes critically to this failure in this setting.
  After correcting these issues, we uncover a distinct emergent pathology: competitive overfitting, where co-adapting agents maintain stable self-play performance while generalisation win rate collapses from $73.5\%$ to $21.6\%$. Critically, this failure is undetectable via standard self-play metrics: both agents co-adapt equally, so the self-play win rate remains near $50\%$ throughout the collapse.
  We propose a minimal intervention -- opponent mixing, where $20\%$ of training episodes substitute a fixed uniformly-random policy for the co-adaptive opponent -- which mitigates competitive overfitting and restores generalisation to $77.1\%$ ($\pm 12.6\%$, $10$ seeds) without population-based training or additional infrastructure. We open-source Territory Paint Wars to provide a reproducible benchmark for studying competitive MARL failure modes.

</details>


### [101] [Enhancing sample efficiency in reinforcement-learning-based flow control: replacing the critic with an adaptive reduced-order model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04986)
*Zesheng Yao,Zhen-Hua Wan,Canjun Yang,Qingchao Xia,Mengqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于自适应降阶模型的强化学习框架，用于主动流动控制，通过ROM估计梯度信息，显著提高样本效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无模型深度强化学习方法样本效率低，需要大量环境交互数据，这在流体控制等复杂系统中成本高昂

Method: 结合线性动力系统和神经常微分方程构建物理启发的ROM，通过算子推断和梯度优化训练，在控制器-环境交互中自适应更新ROM，通过ROM的可微仿真优化控制器

Result: 在Blasius边界层流动中，单次系统辨识即可获得优于传统线性设计、接近DRL性能的控制器；在方柱绕流中，用更少探索数据实现更好的减阻效果

Conclusion: 该ROM-based DRL框架解决了无模型DRL的关键限制，为设计更高效样本的主动流动控制器奠定了基础

Abstract: Model-free deep reinforcement learning (DRL) methods suffer from poor sample efficiency. To overcome this limitation, this work introduces an adaptive reduced-order-model (ROM)-based reinforcement learning framework for active flow control. In contrast to conventional actor--critic architectures, the proposed approach leverages a ROM to estimate the gradient information required for controller optimization. The design of the ROM structure incorporates physical insights. The ROM integrates a linear dynamical system and a neural ordinary differential equation (NODE) for estimating the nonlinearity in the flow. The parameters of the linear component are identified via operator inference, while the NODE is trained in a data-driven manner using gradient-based optimization. During controller--environment interactions, the ROM is continuously updated with newly collected data, enabling adaptive refinement of the model. The controller is then optimized through differentiable simulation of the ROM. The proposed ROM-based DRL framework is validated on two canonical flow control problems: Blasius boundary layer flow and flow past a square cylinder. For the Blasius boundary layer, the proposed method effectively reduces to a single-episode system identification and controller optimization process, yet it yields controllers that outperform traditional linear designs and achieve performance comparable to DRL approaches with minimal data. For the flow past a square cylinder, the proposed method achieves superior drag reduction with significantly fewer exploration data compared with DRL approaches. The work addresses a key component of model-free DRL control algorithms and lays the foundation for designing more sample-efficient DRL-based active flow controllers.

</details>


### [102] [Cross-fitted Proximal Learning for Model-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05185)
*Nishanth Venkatesh,Andreas A. Malikopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种用于混杂部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程的K折交叉拟合桥函数估计方法，通过更高效利用数据改进现有两阶段估计器


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在隐藏混杂变量的离线强化学习环境中，直接从观测数据学习的模型可能存在偏差，特别是在部分可观测系统中，潜在因素可能同时影响动作、奖励和未来观测

Method: 将桥函数学习构建为条件矩限制问题，其中干扰项由条件均值嵌入和条件密度给出；开发K折交叉拟合扩展的两阶段桥估计器，保留原始基于桥的识别策略，同时比单一样本分割更高效利用数据

Result: 推导了交叉拟合估计器的oracle比较器界，并将误差分解为由干扰项估计引起的阶段I项和由经验平均引起的阶段II项

Conclusion: 提出的K折交叉拟合桥估计器在保持识别策略的同时，通过更高效的数据利用改进了混杂部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程中的桥函数统计估计

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning is attractive for sequential decision-making because it explicitly estimates reward and transition models and then supports planning through simulated rollouts. In offline settings with hidden confounding, however, models learned directly from observational data may be biased. This challenge is especially pronounced in partially observable systems, where latent factors may jointly affect actions, rewards, and future observations. Recent work has shown that policy evaluation in such confounded partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) can be reduced to estimating reward-emission and observation-transition bridge functions satisfying conditional moment restrictions (CMRs). In this paper, we study the statistical estimation of these bridge functions. We formulate bridge learning as a CMR problem with nuisance objects given by a conditional mean embedding and a conditional density. We then develop a $K$-fold cross-fitted extension of the existing two-stage bridge estimator. The proposed procedure preserves the original bridge-based identification strategy while using the available data more efficiently than a single sample split. We also derive an oracle-comparator bound for the cross-fitted estimator and decompose the resulting error into a Stage I term induced by nuisance estimation and a Stage II term induced by empirical averaging.

</details>


### [103] [Cactus: Accelerating Auto-Regressive Decoding with Constrained Acceptance Speculative Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04987)
*Yongchang Hao,Lili Mou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Cactus是一种改进的推测采样方法，通过约束优化框架保证与验证器分布的受控偏离，提高接受率而不显著影响输出质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推测采样(SpS)过于严格地要求与验证器LLM分布完全匹配，而典型接受采样(TAS)虽然提高了接受率但会扭曲验证器分布，可能影响输出质量。需要一种既能提高接受率又能控制分布偏离的方法。

Method: 通过约束优化视角形式化推测采样算法，提出Cactus方法，该方法保证与验证器分布的受控偏离，同时提高接受率。基于优化框架设计接受准则。

Result: 在广泛的基准测试中，Cactus方法被证实是有效的，能够在控制分布偏离的同时提高接受率。

Conclusion: Cactus通过约束优化框架改进了推测采样，在保证与验证器分布受控偏离的前提下提高了接受率，平衡了加速效果和输出质量。

Abstract: Speculative sampling (SpS) has been successful in accelerating the decoding throughput of auto-regressive large language models by leveraging smaller draft models. SpS strictly enforces the generated distribution to match that of the verifier LLM. This is unnecessarily restrictive as slight variations of the verifier's distribution, such as sampling with top-$k$ or temperature, would also be acceptable. Typical acceptance sampling (TAS) alleviates this issue by accepting more tokens using entropy-based heuristics. However, this approach distorts the verifier distribution, potentially degrading output quality when the verifier encodes critical information. In this work, we formalize the speculative sampling algorithm through the lens of constrained optimization. Based on this formulation, we propose Cactus (constrained acceptance speculative sampling), a method that guarantees controlled divergence from the verifier distribution and increasing acceptance rates. Empirical results across a wide range of benchmarks confirm the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [104] [FNO$^{\angle θ}$: Extended Fourier neural operator for learning state and optimal control of distributed parameter systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05187)
*Zhexian Li,Ketan Savla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出扩展傅里叶神经算子架构，用于学习偏微分方程系统的状态和线性二次加性最优控制，通过将频率变量从实域扩展到复域来改进FNO性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Ehrenpreis-Palamodov基本原理，线性常系数PDE的状态和最优控制可在复域中表示为积分形式，而传统FNO仅使用实域傅里叶变换，无法完全捕捉这种表示，因此需要扩展FNO架构。

Method: 修改FNO层，将逆傅里叶变换中的频率变量从实域扩展到复域，以捕捉基本原理中的积分表示。该方法利用复域积分表示与FNO卷积算子中指数项的相似性。

Result: 在非线性Burgers方程的状态和最优控制学习任务中，扩展FNO相比传统FNO在训练误差上实现了数量级改进，对非周期边界值的预测更准确。

Conclusion: 通过将FNO的频率变量扩展到复域，能够更好地表示线性PDE的状态和最优控制，显著提升了学习性能和边界条件处理能力。

Abstract: We propose an extended Fourier neural operator (FNO) architecture for learning state and linear quadratic additive optimal control of systems governed by partial differential equations. Using the Ehrenpreis-Palamodov fundamental principle, we show that any state and optimal control of linear PDEs with constant coefficients can be represented as an integral in the complex domain. The integrand of this representation involves the same exponential term as in the inverse Fourier transform, where the latter is used to represent the convolution operator in FNO layer. Motivated by this observation, we modify the FNO layer by extending the frequency variable in the inverse Fourier transform from the real to complex domain to capture the integral representation from the fundamental principle. We illustrate the performance of FNO in learning state and optimal control for the nonlinear Burgers' equation, showing order of magnitude improvements in training errors and more accurate predictions of non-periodic boundary values over FNO.

</details>


### [105] [Learning-Based Multi-Criteria Decision Making Model for Sawmill Location Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04996)
*Mahid Ahmed,Ali Dogru,Chaoyang Zhang,Chao Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于学习的多准则决策框架，结合机器学习与GIS空间分析，用于锯木厂选址优化，在密西西比州案例中识别出10-11%的高适宜区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锯木厂选址对木材供应链的效率、盈利能力和可持续性至关重要，需要数据驱动、无偏且可复现的方法来评估场地适宜性。

Method: 提出LB-MCDM框架，集成机器学习与基于GIS的多准则决策分析。使用五种ML算法（随机森林、支持向量机、XGBoost、逻辑回归、K近邻）识别适宜位置，并采用SHAP技术确定各准则的相对重要性。

Result: 随机森林分类器表现最佳；SHAP分析显示供需比（反映当地市场竞争动态的复合特征）是最重要因素，其次是道路、铁路线和城市区域距离；模型验证表明密西西比州10-11%的土地高度适宜锯木厂选址。

Conclusion: LB-MCDM框架为锯木厂选址提供了有效的数据驱动方法，能够识别关键影响因素并生成可靠的适宜性地图，有助于优化木材供应链的空间规划。

Abstract: Strategically locating a sawmill is vital for enhancing the efficiency, profitability, and sustainability of timber supply chains. Our study proposes a Learning-Based Multi-Criteria Decision-Making (LB-MCDM) framework that integrates machine learning (ML) with GIS-based spatial location analysis via MCDM. The proposed framework provides a data-driven, unbiased, and replicable approach to assessing site suitability. We demonstrate the utility of the proposed model through a case study in Mississippi (MS). We apply five ML algorithms (Random Forest Classifier, Support Vector Classifier, XGBoost Classifier, Logistic Regression, and K-Nearest Neighbors Classifier) to identify the most suitable sawmill locations in Mississippi. Among these models, the Random Forest Classifier achieved the highest performance. We use the SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations) technique to determine the relative importance of each criterion, revealing the Supply-Demand Ratio, a composite feature that reflects local market competition dynamics, as the most influential factor, followed by Road, Rail Line and Urban Area Distance. The validation of suitability maps generated by our LB-MCDM model suggests that 10-11% of the MS landscape is highly suitable for sawmill location.

</details>


### [106] [El Nino Prediction Based on Weather Forecast and Geographical Time-series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04998)
*Viet Trinh,Ha-Vy Luu,Quoc-Khiem Nguyen-Pham,Hung Tong,Thanh-Huyen Tran,Hoai-Nam Nguyen Dang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个结合CNN和LSTM的混合深度学习框架，用于提高厄尔尼诺事件的预测精度和提前时间


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统厄尔尼诺预测模型主要依赖海洋和大气指数，缺乏对全面气象和地理数据集的细粒度特征和动态相互作用的捕捉，限制了预测准确性和提前时间

Method: 整合实时全球天气预报数据、异常值、次表层海洋热含量和不同时空分辨率的大气压数据，采用CNN进行空间特征提取和LSTM进行时间依赖性建模的混合深度学习架构

Result: 论文摘要未提供具体实验结果，但框架旨在识别厄尔尼诺事件的复杂前兆和演化模式

Conclusion: 提出的混合深度学习框架有望通过整合多源数据和捕捉时空特征，改善厄尔尼诺事件的预测能力，从而减轻其全球气候、经济和社会影响

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel framework for enhancing the prediction accuracy and lead time of El Niño events, crucial for mitigating their global climatic, economic, and societal impacts. Traditional prediction models often rely on oceanic and atmospheric indices, which may lack the granularity or dynamic interplay captured by comprehensive meteorological and geographical datasets. Our framework integrates real-time global weather forecast data with anomalies, subsurface ocean heat content, and atmospheric pressure across various temporal and spatial resolutions. Leveraging a hybrid deep learning architecture that combines a Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) for spatial feature extraction and a Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) network for temporal dependency modeling, the framework aims to identify complex precursors and evolving patterns of El Niño events.

</details>


### [107] [PRIME: Prototype-Driven Multimodal Pretraining for Cancer Prognosis with Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04999)
*Kai Yu,Shuang Zhou,Yiran Song,Zaifu Zhan,Jie Peng,Kaixiong Zhou,Tianlong Chen,Feng Xie,Meng Wang,Huazhu Fu,Mingquan Lin,Rui Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRIME是一种缺失感知的多模态自监督预训练框架，用于从部分观测的临床队列中学习稳健可迁移表示，支持任意模态子集的癌症预后预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态自监督预训练方法需要完全配对和完整的输入，而实际临床队列通常是碎片化的，经常缺失一种或多种模态，这限制了监督融合和可扩展的多模态预训练。

Method: PRIME将异质模态嵌入映射到统一的标记空间，引入共享原型记忆库通过患者级共识检索进行潜在空间语义填补，产生结构对齐的标记而不重建原始信号。采用两种互补的预训练目标：结构化缺失增强下的模态间对齐和后融合一致性。

Result: 在TCGA数据集上，PRIME在32种癌症类型上进行无标签预训练，在五个队列的下游5折评估中，在总体生存预测、3年死亡率分类和3年复发分类三个任务上分别达到0.653 C-index、0.689 AUROC和0.637 AUROC的最佳宏观平均性能。

Conclusion: 缺失感知的多模态预训练是在碎片化临床数据设置中进行预后建模的实用策略，PRIME在测试时缺失情况下提高了鲁棒性，并支持参数高效和标签高效的适应。

Abstract: Multimodal self-supervised pretraining offers a promising route to cancer prognosis by integrating histopathology whole-slide images, gene expression, and pathology reports, yet most existing approaches require fully paired and complete inputs. In practice, clinical cohorts are fragmented and often miss one or more modalities, limiting both supervised fusion and scalable multimodal pretraining. We propose PRIME, a missing-aware multimodal self-supervised pretraining framework that learns robust and transferable representations from partially observed cohorts. PRIME maps heterogeneous modality embeddings into a unified token space and introduces a shared prototype memory bank for latent-space semantic imputation via patient-level consensus retrieval, producing structurally aligned tokens without reconstructing raw signals. Two complementary pretraining objectives: inter-modality alignment and post-fusion consistency under structured missingness augmentation, jointly learn representations that remain predictive under arbitrary modality subsets. We evaluate PRIME on The Cancer Genome Atlas with label-free pretraining on 32 cancer types and downstream 5-fold evaluation on five cohorts across overall survival prediction, 3-year mortality classification, and 3-year recurrence classification. PRIME achieves the best macro-average performance among all compared methods, reaching 0.653 C-index, 0.689 AUROC, and 0.637 AUROC on the three tasks, respectively, while improving robustness under test-time missingness and supporting parameter-efficient and label-efficient adaptation. These results support missing-aware multimodal pretraining as a practical strategy for prognosis modeling in fragmented clinical data settings.

</details>


### [108] [Blind-Spot Mass: A Good-Turing Framework for Quantifying Deployment Coverage Risk in Machine Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05057)
*Biplab Pal,Santanu Bhattacharya,Madanjit Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 盲点质量是一种基于Good-Turing框架的机器学习部署覆盖风险量化方法，用于估计在有限训练数据中结构性地未充分支持的罕见状态的总概率质量，揭示标准测试集准确性与实际部署可靠性之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代ML系统的操作状态分布通常具有重尾特性，这意味着大量有效但罕见的状态在有限的训练和评估数据中结构性地未得到充分支持。这导致"覆盖盲区"问题：模型在标准测试集上可能表现准确，但在部署状态空间的大片区域中仍不可靠。

Method: 提出盲点质量B_n(τ)作为部署指标，使用Good-Turing未见过物种估计方法，计算经验支持度低于阈值τ的状态的总概率质量。进一步推导出覆盖强加的准确率上限，将整体性能分解为支持部分和盲点部分，区分容量限制与数据限制。

Result: 在可穿戴人类活动识别(HAR)和MIMIC-IV医院数据库(275例入院)中验证了该框架。在两种应用中，盲点质量曲线在τ=5时都收敛到相同的95%。跨结构独立领域(不同模态、特征空间、标签空间和应用)的复制表明盲点质量是量化组合覆盖风险的通用ML方法。

Conclusion: 盲点分解能够识别哪些活动或临床状态主导风险，为工业从业者提供有针对性的数据收集、归一化/重归一化以及基于物理或领域知识的约束等可操作指导，以实现更安全的部署。

Abstract: Blind-spot mass is a Good-Turing framework for quantifying deployment coverage risk in machine learning. In modern ML systems, operational state distributions are often heavy-tailed, implying that a long tail of valid but rare states is structurally under-supported in finite training and evaluation data. This creates a form of 'coverage blindness': models can appear accurate on standard test sets yet remain unreliable across large regions of the deployment state space.
  We propose blind-spot mass B_n(tau), a deployment metric estimating the total probability mass assigned to states whose empirical support falls below a threshold tau. B_n(tau) is computed using Good-Turing unseen-species estimation and yields a principled estimate of how much of the operational distribution lies in reliability-critical, under-supported regimes. We further derive a coverage-imposed accuracy ceiling, decomposing overall performance into supported and blind components and separating capacity limits from data limits.
  We validate the framework in wearable human activity recognition (HAR) using wrist-worn inertial data. We then replicate the same analysis in the MIMIC-IV hospital database with 275 admissions, where the blind-spot mass curve converges to the same 95% at tau = 5 across clinical state abstractions. This replication across structurally independent domains - differing in modality, feature space, label space, and application - shows that blind-spot mass is a general ML methodology for quantifying combinatorial coverage risk, not an application-specific artifact.
  Blind-spot decomposition identifies which activities or clinical regimes dominate risk, providing actionable guidance for industrial practitioners on targeted data collection, normalization/renormalization, and physics- or domain-informed constraints for safer deployment.

</details>


### [109] [Dynamic Linear Coregionalization for Realistic Synthetic Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05064)
*Annita Vapsi,Penghang Liu,Saheed Obitayo,Aakriti,Manoj Cherukumalli,Prathamesh Patil,Amit Varshney,Nicolas Marchesotti,Elizabeth Fons,Vamsi K. Potluru,Manuela Veloso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DynLMC模型通过动态线性协同区域化方法生成具有时变相关性和跨通道滞后结构的合成时间序列数据，提升基础模型的零样本预测性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列合成数据生成器通常假设静态相关性，缺乏真实的跨通道依赖关系，这限制了时间序列基础模型的训练效果和可迁移性

Method: 提出DynLMC（动态线性协同区域化模型），该模型结合时变、机制切换的相关性和跨通道滞后结构，能够生成具有真实相关动态的多元时间序列合成数据

Result: 在DynLMC生成的数据上微调三个基础模型，在九个基准测试中均获得一致的零样本预测性能提升，证明了动态跨通道相关性建模的有效性

Conclusion: 建模动态跨通道相关性能够增强时间序列基础模型的可迁移性，突出了以数据为中心的预训练方法的重要性

Abstract: Synthetic data is essential for training foundation models for time series (FMTS), but most generators assume static correlations, and are typically missing realistic inter-channel dependencies. We introduce DynLMC, a Dynamic Linear Model of Coregionalization, that incorporates time-varying, regime-switching correlations and cross-channel lag structures. Our approach produces synthetic multivariate time series with correlation dynamics that closely resemble real data. Fine-tuning three foundational models on DynLMC-generated data yields consistent zero-shot forecasting improvements across nine benchmarks. Our results demonstrate that modeling dynamic inter-channel correlations enhances FMTS transferability, highlighting the importance of data-centric pretraining.

</details>


### [110] [Towards Scaling Law Analysis For Spatiotemporal Weather Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05068)
*Alexander Kiefer,Prasanna Balaprakash,Xiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究扩展了神经缩放分析到自回归天气预报领域，从单步训练损失扩展到长时滚动预测和逐通道指标，揭示了跨通道和跨时间尺度的异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天气预报难以用NLP和CV中相对成熟的计算最优缩放定律来表征，因为自回归滚动会累积长期误差，输出耦合多个物理通道且尺度不同，全局聚合的测试指标可能与逐通道、长期行为不一致。

Method: 扩展神经缩放分析框架，从单步训练损失扩展到长时滚动预测和逐通道指标。量化：(1)预测误差在通道间的分布及其随预测时间尺度的增长速率；(2)全局聚合误差下幂律缩放是否成立；(3)参数、数据和计算三个缩放维度上，缩放拟合如何随时间尺度和通道变化。

Result: 发现强烈的跨通道和跨时间尺度异质性：全局聚合的缩放可能看起来有利，但许多通道在长期预测中性能下降。不同通道的误差分布和增长模式存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 研究结果对加权目标函数、时间尺度感知的课程学习策略以及跨输出资源的分配具有重要启示。需要更精细的缩放分析来考虑天气预报特有的跨通道和跨时间尺度异质性。

Abstract: Compute-optimal scaling laws are relatively well studied for NLP and CV, where objectives are typically single-step and targets are comparatively homogeneous. Weather forecasting is harder to characterize in the same framework: autoregressive rollouts compound errors over long horizons, outputs couple many physical channels with disparate scales and predictability, and globally pooled test metrics can disagree sharply with per-channel, late-lead behavior implied by short-horizon training. We extend neural scaling analysis for autoregressive weather forecasting from single-step training loss to long rollouts and per-channel metrics. We quantify (1) how prediction error is distributed across channels and how its growth rate evolves with forecast horizon, (2) if power law scaling holds for test error, relative to rollout length when error is pooled globally, and (3) how that fit varies jointly with horizon and channel for parameter, data, and compute-based scaling axes. We find strong cross-channel and cross-horizon heterogeneity: pooled scaling can look favorable while many channels degrade at late leads. We discuss implications for weighted objectives, horizon-aware curricula, and resource allocation across outputs.

</details>


### [111] [Hierarchical SVG Tokenization: Learning Compact Visual Programs for Scalable Vector Graphics Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05072)
*Ximing Xing,Ziteng Xue,Zhenxi Li,Weicong Liang,Linqing Wang,Zhantao Yang,Tiankai Hang,Zijin Yin,Qinglin Lu,Chunyu Wang,Qian Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HiVG提出了一种针对SVG生成的分层tokenization框架，通过原子token和几何约束的segment token来改进向量图形的自回归生成，解决了传统字节级tokenization在几何结构表示上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型在SVG生成中仍使用从自然语言处理继承的通用字节级tokenization，这种tokenization无法有效反映向量图形的几何结构。数值坐标被分割成离散符号，破坏了空间关系并引入严重的token冗余，导致坐标幻觉和低效的长序列生成。

Method: HiVG框架包含：1) 将原始SVG字符串分解为结构化原子token；2) 将可执行的命令-参数组压缩为几何约束的segment token；3) 引入分层均值-噪声初始化策略，向新token嵌入注入数值排序信号和语义先验；4) 采用渐进式课程训练范式，逐步增加程序复杂度。

Result: 在文本到SVG和图像到SVG任务上的广泛实验表明，与传统tokenization方案相比，HiVG在生成保真度、空间一致性和序列效率方面均有显著改进。

Conclusion: HiVG通过分层tokenization框架有效解决了SVG生成中的几何结构表示问题，提高了自回归向量图形生成的效率和准确性，为可执行SVG程序的稳定学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Recent large language models have shifted SVG generation from differentiable rendering optimization to autoregressive program synthesis. However, existing approaches still rely on generic byte-level tokenization inherited from natural language processing, which poorly reflects the geometric structure of vector graphics. Numerical coordinates are fragmented into discrete symbols, destroying spatial relationships and introducing severe token redundancy, often leading to coordinate hallucination and inefficient long-sequence generation. To address these challenges, we propose HiVG, a hierarchical SVG tokenization framework tailored for autoregressive vector graphics generation. HiVG decomposes raw SVG strings into structured \textit{atomic tokens} and further compresses executable command--parameter groups into geometry-constrained \textit{segment tokens}, substantially improving sequence efficiency while preserving syntactic validity. To further mitigate spatial mismatch, we introduce a Hierarchical Mean--Noise (HMN) initialization strategy that injects numerical ordering signals and semantic priors into new token embeddings. Combined with a curriculum training paradigm that progressively increases program complexity, HiVG enables more stable learning of executable SVG programs. Extensive experiments on both text-to-SVG and image-to-SVG tasks demonstrate improved generation fidelity, spatial consistency, and sequence efficiency compared with conventional tokenization schemes.

</details>


### [112] [Vintix II: Decision Pre-Trained Transformer is a Scalable In-Context Reinforcement Learner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05112)
*Andrei Polubarov,Lyubaykin Nikita,Alexander Derevyagin,Artyom Grishin,Igor Saprygin,Aleksandr Serkov,Mark Averchenko,Daniil Tikhonov,Maksim Zhdanov,Alexander Nikulin,Ilya Zisman,Albina Klepach,Alexey Zemtsov,Vladislav Kurenkov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将决策预训练Transformer（DPT）扩展到多领域环境，使用流匹配训练，在数百个任务上获得更好的泛化性能，优于之前的算法蒸馏方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然算法蒸馏（AD）开创了上下文强化学习范式并在多领域环境中进行了扩展，但其泛化能力有限；决策预训练Transformer（DPT）在简化领域中表现出更强的上下文强化学习能力，但可扩展性尚未得到验证

Method: 将DPT扩展到多样化的多领域环境，采用流匹配作为训练方法，将其解释为贝叶斯后验采样，在数百个不同任务上进行训练

Result: 获得了一个在数百个不同任务上训练的智能体，在保留测试集上实现了明显的泛化增益，优于之前的AD扩展方法，在在线和离线推理中都表现出更强的性能

Conclusion: 这项工作强化了上下文强化学习作为训练通用智能体的可行替代方案，相对于专家蒸馏方法具有优势

Abstract: Recent progress in in-context reinforcement learning (ICRL) has demonstrated its potential for training generalist agents that can acquire new tasks directly at inference. Algorithm Distillation (AD) pioneered this paradigm and was subsequently scaled to multi-domain settings, although its ability to generalize to unseen tasks remained limited. The Decision Pre-Trained Transformer (DPT) was introduced as an alternative, showing stronger in-context reinforcement learning abilities in simplified domains, but its scalability had not been established. In this work, we extend DPT to diverse multi-domain environments, applying Flow Matching as a natural training choice that preserves its interpretation as Bayesian posterior sampling. As a result, we obtain an agent trained across hundreds of diverse tasks that achieves clear gains in generalization to the held-out test set. This agent improves upon prior AD scaling and demonstrates stronger performance in both online and offline inference, reinforcing ICRL as a viable alternative to expert distillation for training generalist agents.

</details>


### [113] [Reasoning Through Chess: How Reasoning Evolves from Data Through Fine-Tuning and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05134)
*Lucas Dionisopoulos,Nicklas Majamaki,Prithviraj Ammanabrolu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究语言模型在棋类任务中的推理演化，发现直接预测最佳走法的微调能带来最强性能但产生不忠实推理，而多步轨迹训练能获得相当性能且保持忠实推理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索语言模型如何在其原生能力不足的任务中进行推理，研究从监督微调到强化学习过程中推理能力的演化，特别关注棋类任务中不同训练策略对推理质量和性能的影响

Method: 使用理论启发的数据集分析语言模型在棋类任务中的表现，比较两种微调策略：1）直接预测最佳走法；2）多步轨迹训练。随后进行强化学习，并评估推理忠实性、走法质量分布变化和幻觉率

Result: 直接预测最佳走法的微调带来最强的强化学习效果和下游性能，但产生不忠实推理；多步轨迹训练获得相当性能且保持忠实推理，强化学习能显著提升走法质量分布并降低幻觉率；多个监督微调检查点指标可预测强化学习后模型性能

Conclusion: 训练策略选择需要在性能与推理忠实性之间权衡，多步轨迹训练提供更稳定强化学习和忠实推理的平衡方案，监督微调阶段的指标对最终模型性能有预测价值，7B参数模型在棋类任务中超越了领先的开源推理模型

Abstract: How can you get a language model to reason in a task it natively struggles with? We study how reasoning evolves in a language model -- from supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to reinforcement learning (RL) -- by analyzing how a set of theoretically-inspired datasets impacts language model performance in chess. We find that fine-tuning a model to directly predict the best move leads to effective RL and the strongest downstream performance -- however, the RL step elicits unfaithful reasoning (reasoning inconsistent with the chosen move). Alternatively, training on multi-move trajectories yields comparable downstream performance with faithful reasoning and more stable RL. We show that RL induces a substantial positive shift in the distribution of move quality and reduces hallucination rates as a side effect. Finally, we find several SFT-checkpoint metrics -- metrics spanning evaluation performance, hallucination rates, and reasoning quality -- to be predictive of post-RL model performance. We release checkpoints and final models as well as training data, evaluations, and code which allowed us to surpass leading open-source reasoning models in chess with a 7B-parameter model.

</details>


### [114] [Not All Turns Are Equally Hard: Adaptive Thinking Budgets For Efficient Multi-Turn Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05164)
*Neharika Jali,Anupam Nayak,Gauri Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TAB是一种用于多轮推理的自适应预算分配策略，通过将多轮推理建模为序列计算分配问题，在保持准确性的同时节省高达35-40%的推理token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM推理性能趋于平稳，提高推理时计算效率变得至关重要。现有方法主要关注单轮设置，未能解决多轮推理中的序列依赖性问题，导致简单查询过度思考和长推理链问题。

Method: 将多轮推理建模为序列计算分配问题，形式化为多目标马尔可夫决策过程。提出TAB（Turn-Adaptive Budgets）预算分配策略，通过Group Relative Policy Optimization训练，学习在尊重全局每问题token约束的同时最大化任务准确性。TAB All-SubQ变体在已知所有子问题计划的情况下，基于对话历史和所有过去及未来子问题进行token预算分配。

Result: 在数学推理基准测试中，TAB实现了优越的准确性-token权衡，节省高达35%的token同时保持准确性优于静态和现成LLM预算基线。TAB All-SubQ在已知所有子问题的情况下节省高达40%的token。

Conclusion: TAB通过自适应预算分配有效解决了多轮推理中的计算效率问题，为简单轮次分配较小预算，为关键困难推理步骤保留适当token，显著提升了推理效率。

Abstract: As LLM reasoning performance plateau, improving inference-time compute efficiency is crucial to mitigate overthinking and long thinking traces even for simple queries.
